Headache (शिरः अर्ति/सिर दर्द), weakness of sense organs (इन्द्रिय दौर्बल्य/इंद्रिय कमजोरी), stiffness of neck (मन्यास्तम्भ/गर्दन अकड़ना), and facial paralysis (अर्दितम्/लकवा) arise from suppressing sneezing (क्षुतेः/छींक).
By using sharp smoking (तीक्ष्ण धूम/तेज बीड़ी), collyrium (अञ्जन/काजल), smelling (आघ्राण/सूंघना), nasal drops (नावन/नस्य), and looking at the sun (अर्क विलोकनैः/सूरज देखना).
One should induce the obstructed sneeze (क्षुतिम् सक्ताम्/रुकी हुई छींक), and should practice (शीलयेत्/आदत डालना) sweating and oil massage (स्वेद अभ्यङ्गौ/सेक और मालिश).
And food that pacifies Vata (वातघ्नम् अन्नम्/गैस नाशक भोजन) should be used, along with ghee (घृतम्/घी) after meals (उत्तर भक्तकम्/खाने के बाद).
Emaciation (शोष/सूखा रोग), body weakness (अङ्गसाद/थकान), deafness (बाधिर्य/बहरापन), delusion (संमोह/बेहोशी), dizziness (भ्रम/चक्कर), and heart diseases (हृद् गदाः/दिल के रोग) occur.
From the suppression of thirst (तृष्णायाः निग्रहात्/प्यास रोकना); in that condition, all cooling treatments (शीतः विधिः/ठंडा उपचार) are beneficial (हितः/फायदेमंद).
Body ache (अङ्ग भङ्ग/बदन दर्द), loss of appetite (अरुचि/अरुचि), exhaustion (ग्लानि/थकान), leanness (कार्श्य/दुबलापन), colic pain (शूल/पेट दर्द), and dizziness (भ्रमाः/चक्कर) arise from hunger suppression (क्षुधः/भूख).
In that condition, food (भोजनम्/खाना) that is light (लघु/हल्का), unctuous (स्निग्धम्/चिकना), warm (उष्णम्/गर्म), and in small quantity (अल्पम्/थोड़ा) should be used.
From suppression of sleep (निद्रायाः/नींद रोकना), delusion (मोह/मूर्खता), fainting (मूर्च्छा/बेहोशी), heaviness in eyes (अक्षि गौरव/आंखों का भारीपन), laziness (आलस्य/सुस्ती), and yawning (जृम्भिका/उबासी) occur.
Along with body aches (अङ्ग मर्दः/बदन दर्द); in that condition, deep sleep (स्वप्नः/नींद) and gentle massaging (संवाहनानि/हाथ-पैर दबाना) are desired (इष्टः/पसंदीदा).
By the suppression of cough (कासस्य रोधात्/खांसी रोकना), its increase, breathlessness (श्वास/दमा), loss of appetite (अरुचि/अरुचि), and heart diseases (हृद् आमयाः/दिल के रोग) occur.
Wasting (शोषः/सूखना) and hiccups (हिध्मा/हिचकी) also occur, and here treatments that cure cough (कासहा विधिः/खांसी नाशक उपाय) should be highly employed (सुतराम्/विशेष रूप से).
Phantom tumor (गुल्म/गांठ), heart disease (हृद्रोग/दिल की बीमारी), and confusion (संमोहाः/बेहोशी) occur from suppressed breathlessness due to exertion (श्रम श्वासात् विधारितात्/थकान की सांस रोकना).
Resting (विश्रमणम्/आराम) is beneficial there, along with Vata-alleviating treatment procedures (वातघ्नः क्रिया क्रमः/वायु नाशक चिकित्सा).
From suppression of yawning (जृम्भायाः/उबासी), diseases similar to sneezing arise, and all treatments that conquer Vata (अनिल जित् विधिः/वायु नाशक उपाय) should be done.
Chronic rhinitis (पीनस/पुरानी सर्दी), eye pain (अक्षि रुक/आंख दर्द), headache (शिरो रुक्/सिर दर्द), heart pain (हृद् रुक्/दिल का दर्द), neck stiffness (मन्यास्तम्भ/गर्दन अकड़ना), loss of appetite (अरुचि/अरुचि), and dizziness (भ्रमाः/चक्कर) occur.
Along with abdominal tumors (सगुल्माः/गांठ) from suppressing tears (बाष्पतः/आंसू), and there sleep (स्वप्नः/नींद), wine (मद्यम्/शराब), and pleasing stories (प्रियाः कथाः/मनपसंद बातें) are recommended.
Erysipelas (विसर्प/त्वचा रोग), urticaria (कोठ/पित्ती), skin diseases (कुष्ठ/कोढ़), eye itching (अक्षि कण्डू/आंख की खुजली), anemia (पाण्डु आमय/खून की कमी), and fever (ज्वराः/बुखार) occur.
Along with cough (सकास/खांसी), asthma (श्वास/दमा), nausea (हृल्लास/जी मिचलाना), pigmentation (व्यङ्ग/झाइयां), and swelling (श्वयथवः/सूजन) from suppression of vomiting (वमेः/उल्टी).
Gargling (गण्डूष/कुल्ला), therapeutic smoking (धूम/धूम्रपान), fasting (उपवास/व्रत), dry food (रूक्षम् आहारम्/रूखा भोजन), and then vomiting it out (भुक्त्वा तत् उद्वमः/खाकर उल्टी करना) are advised.
Physical exercise (व्यायामः/कसरत), bloodletting (श्रुतिः अस्त्रस्य/खून निकालना), and purgation therapy (विरेचनम्/पेट साफ करना) are highly praised here.
Oil mixed with alkali (सक्षार/क्षार युक्त) and salt (लवणम्/नमक) is recommended for massage (अभ्यङ्ग अर्थम्/मालिश के लिए).
Pain in the semen passage (शुक आन्त्र श्रवणम्/वीर्य मार्ग दर्द), groin pain (गुह्य वेदना/गुप्तांग का दर्द), swelling (श्वयथु/सूजन), and fever (ज्वराः/बुखार) occur.
Heart pain (हृद् व्यथा/दिल का दर्द), urinary retention (मूत्र सङ्गौ/पेशाब रुकना), body aches (अङ्ग भङ्ग/बदन दर्द), hernia (वृद्धि/अण्डकोश बढ़ना), urinary stones (अश्मरी/पथरी), and impotence (षण्ढताः/नपुंसकता) arise from suppression of semen.
Rooster meat (ताम्रचूड/मुर्गा), alcohol (सुरा/शराब), Shali rice (शालि/चावल), enema (बस्ति/एनिमा), oil massage (अभ्यङ्ग/मालिश), and tub bath (अवगाहनम्/टब बाथ) are indicated.
And milk (क्षीरम्/दूध) prepared with bladder-cleansing herbs (बस्ति शुद्धिकरैः सिद्धम्/मूत्राशय साफ करने वाली दवाएं) and pleasing women (प्रियाः स्त्रियः/मनपसंद स्त्रियां) should be resorted to.
The physician should reject (त्यजेत्/छोड़ देना) a patient afflicted with thirst and colic pain (तृट् शूल आर्तम्/प्यास-दर्द से पीड़ित), emaciated (क्षीणम्/कमजोर), and vomiting feces (विड् भ्रमम्/मल की उल्टी) due to urge suppression (वेग रोधिनम्/वेग रोकने वाला).
Indeed, all diseases (सर्वे अपि रोगाः/सारे रोग भी) are born from the premature forcing or suppression of natural urges (वेग उदीरण धारणैः/वेगों को लाना या रोकना).
The general remedy (साधनम्/उपचार) is specified there for those urges, mostly against Vata (ये तु तान् प्रति/उनके लिए).
Because due to that suppression, the bodily air (पवनः/वायु) generally gets aggravated (प्रकुप्यति/बिगड़ जाता है) in many ways (अनेकाधा/कई प्रकार से).
Therefore, the afflicted person (आर्तः/मरीज) should use food, drinks, and medicines (अन्न पान औषधम्/खान-पान-दवाई) that normalize the downward movement of Vata (अनुलोमनम्/वायु अनुकूल करने वाला).
For those various disorders (तेषु तेषु विकारेषु/उन-उन रोगों में), treatment should be done according to their respective nature (यथास्वम् च चिकित्सितम्/अपने-अपने अनुसार इलाज).
Just as mud (पङ्कः/कीचड़) will definitely form (अवश्यम् भवेत्/जरूर होगा) over time even on a clean crystal gem (अपङ्के अपि मणौ/बिना कीचड़ वाली मणि पर), because of neglect.
Therefore, one should rise at the proper time (यथा कालम्/सही समय पर) towards the purification of waste products (मलानाम् शोधनम् प्रति/मलों की सफाई के लिए).
If neglected (उपेक्षितः/ध्यान न देना), those wastes reach the peak of accumulation (चय काष्ठाम् उपारुह्य/इकट्ठा होने की हद पार करना) and cause diseases.
Often, even after a very long time (सुचिरेण अपि/बहुत समय बाद भी), they create diseases that resist medicines (भेषजष द्वेषिणः गदान्/दवाई से ठीक न होने वाले रोग).
Such as extreme obesity (अतिस्थौल्य/बहुत मोटापा), low digestive fire (अग्नि सदन/मंदाग्नि), skin diseases (कुष्ठ/कोढ़), diabetes (मेह/प्रमेह), and loss of vital energy (हृत ओजसः/ओज का नाश).
Obstruction of channels (स्रोतो रोध/नसों का रुकना), loss of vision focus (अक्षि विभ्रंश/आंखों की कमजोरी), asthma (श्वास/दमा), swelling (श्वयथु/सूजन), and anemia (पाण्डुताः/पीलिया या खून की कमी).
Delusion (आमोह/बेहोशी), stiffness (स्तम्भ/जकड़न), enlargement of abdomen (जठर उच्छ्राय/पेट बढ़ना या जलोदर), sluggish bowels (अलसक/पेट का धीमा होना), and rigid body paralysis (दण्डकान्/शरीर का डंडे जैसा अकड़ना).
Excessive satiety (तृप्ति/बिना खाए पेट भरा लगना), drowsiness (प्रमीलक/झपकी), laziness (आलस्य/सुस्ती), irritable bowel syndrome (ग्रहणी/संग्रहणी), piles (अर्शः/बवासीर), and fistula (भगन्दरान्/भगंदर).
Splenomegaly (प्लीह/तिल्ली बढ़ना), abscess (विद्रधि/फोड़ा), erysipelas (विसर्प/त्वचा का फैलने वाला रोग), intoxication (मद/नशा), coma (संन्यास/गहरी बेहोशी), and chronic rhinitis (पीनसान्/पुरानी सर्दी).
Vomiting (छर्दि/उल्टी), goiter (गण्ड/गले की गांठ), worms (क्रमि/कीड़े), compact tumor masses (ग्रथित/गांठें), sleepiness (तन्द्रालुः/उंघना), and excessive dreaming (स्वप्न दर्शनम्/सपने देखना).
Throat disorders (कण्ठ आमयान्/गले की बीमारियां), headache (मूर्ध रुजम्/सिर दर्द), and destruction (प्रणाशम्/नाश) of intellect and sense organs (बुद्धि इन्द्रियोः/दिमाग और इंद्रियां).
And also of glow (तेजः/चमक), complexion (वर्ण/रंग), and strength (बलानाम्/ताकत), even while satiating the body with nourishing therapies (बृंहणैः अपि/ताकत देने वाले उपचारों से).
And because of this, even with wholesome foods (उचितैः अपि च आहारैः/सही खान-पान से भी), these qualities do not increase (वर्धन्ते न/नहीं बढ़ते).
Since the mouths of nutrient-carrying vessels (रसवहाः शिराः/रस ले जाने वाली नसें) are coated with morbid humors (दोष उपलिप्त वदनाः/दोषों से ढके हुए मुंह वाली).
Therefore, one should administer (युञ्जीत/प्रयोग करना) emesis or elimination therapy (वमनात् दानात्/उल्टी आदि देना) for a healthy person (स्वस्थस्य एव/स्वस्थ व्यक्ति के लिए ही) according to prescribed rules (यथा विधि/विधि के अनुसार).
The bodily humors (दोषाः/वात-पित्त-कफ) subdued by fasting and digestive therapies (जिताः लङ्घन पाचनैः/लंघन और पाचन से जीते हुए) can sometimes flare up again (कदाचित् कुप्यन्ति/कभी-कभी फिर बिगड़ जाते हैं).
But those purified by bio-purification therapies (संशोधनैः शुद्धाः/पंचकर्म से साफ हुए) never have a recurrence (न तेषाम् पुनः उद्भवः/उनका दोबारा जन्म नहीं होता).
Thereafter (अतः ऊर्ध्वम्/इसके बाद), according to proper sequence (यथा क्रमम्/क्रम के अनुसार) and proper suitability (यथा योगम्/योग्यता के अनुसार), one should administer.
Proven rejuvenators (रसायनानि सिद्धानि/रसायन दवाइयां) and aphrodisiac formulations (वृष्य योगान् च/ताकत बढ़ाने वाले योग), being the knower of right time (कालवित्/समय का ज्ञाता).
For a person emaciated by medicines (भेषज क्षपिते/दवाई से कमजोर हुए व्यक्ति के लिए), nourishing wholesome diets (पथ्यम् आहारैः बृंहणम्/फायदेमंद ताकतवर भोजन) should be given gradually (क्रमात्/धीरे-धीरे).
With Shali rice (शालि/चावल), Shashtika rice (षष्टिक/साठी चावल), wheat (गोधूम/गेहूं), green gram (मुद्ग/मूंग दाल), ghee (घृत/घी), and others (आदिभिः/इत्यादि).
Combined with combinations of heart-pleasing (हृद्य/मनपसंद) and digestive-fire-kindling medicines (दीपन भैषज्य/भूख बढ़ाने वाली दवाइयां) that provide taste (रुचि/स्वाद) and digestion (पक्ति दैः/पाचन देने वाले).
Along with oil massage (साभ्यङ्ग/मालिश), herbal paste rubbing (उद्वर्तन/उबटन), bath (स्नान/नहाना), non-unctuous enemas (निरूह/काढ़े का एनिमा), and unctuous enemas (स्नेह बस्तिभिः/तेल का एनिमा).
By doing so, he (सः/वह व्यक्ति) attains happiness (शर्म/सुख) and the complete sharpness of all digestive fires (सर्व पावक पाटवम्/सब प्रकार की भूख का तेज होना).
And clarity of intellect, complexion, and sense organs (धी वर्ण इन्द्रिय वैमल्यम्/बुद्धि-रंग-इंद्रियों का साफ होना), virility (वृषताम्/मर्दानगी), and longevity of life (दैर्घ्यम् आयुषः/लंबी उम्र).
Those diseases which are caused by evil spirits (भूत/अदृश्य शक्तियां), poisons (विष/जहर), wind (वायु/तेज हवा), fire (अग्नि/आग), trauma (क्षत/चोट), and fractures (भङ्ग/हड्डी टूटना) etc.
And also those caused by desire (काम/वासना), anger (क्रोध/गुस्सा), fear (भय/डर), and others (आद्याः च/इत्यादि), are known as exogenous diseases (आगन्तवः गदाः/बाहरी कारणों से होने वाले रोग).
Avoidance of intellectual errors (त्यागः प्रज्ञापराधानाम्/गलतियों को छोड़ना), calming of the senses (इन्द्रिय उपशमः/इंद्रियों को शांत करना), memory (स्मृतिः/याददाश्त).
Knowledge of place, time, and self (देश काल आत्म विज्ञानम्/जगह-समय-स्वयं का ज्ञान), and following righteous conduct (सवृत्तस्य अनुवर्तनम्/अच्छे आचरण का पालन).
Auspicious rites prescribed in Atharva Veda (अथर्व विहिता शान्तिः/वेदोक्त शांति पाठ), and worship of unfavorable planets (प्रतिकूल ग्रह अर्चनम्/ग्रह पूजा).
And methods to avoid contact with evil forces (भूत आदि अस्पर्शन उपायः/भूत-प्रेत आदि से बचने के उपाय) have been specified separately (निर्दिष्टः च पृथक् पृथक्/अलग-अलग कहे गए हैं).
In brief (समासेन/संक्षेप में), this procedure (विधिः एषः/यह नियम) has been shown (प्रदर्शितः/बताया गया है) for non-production (अनुत्पत्त्यै/पैदा न होने के लिए).
Of endogenous and exogenous disorders (निज आगन्तु विकाराणाम्/भीतरी और बाहरी रोगों के), as well as for the pacification (शान्तये/शांति के लिए) of those already manifested (उत्पन्नानाम् च/पैदा हुए रोगों के).
By purifying the accumulation of humors caused by cold (शीत उद्भवम् दोष चयम्/ठंड से इकट्ठा हुआ कफ दोष) in spring (वसन्ते/बसंत ऋतु में), and that caused by summer (ग्रीष्मजम्/गर्मी से बढ़ा पित्त) during the unseasonal period.
And the rainy-season accumulation (वार्षिकम्/बरसात का बढ़ा वात) quickly and properly at the end of autumn clouds (घन अत्यये/शरद ऋतु में), one never gets diseases born from changing seasons (ऋतु जान् रोगान्/मौसम के रोग).
He who daily consumes wholesome food and lifestyle (नित्यम् हित आहार विहार सेवी/रोज अच्छा खाना-घूमना करने वाला), acts after proper examination (समीक्ष्यकारी/सोच-समझकर करने वाला), is unattached to sensory pleasures (विषयेषु असक्तः/भोगों में न फंसने वाला).
Is a donor (दाता/दान देने वाला), impartial (समः/बराबर भाव रखने वाला), devoted to truth (सत्यपरः/सच्चा), forgiving (क्षमावान्/माफ करने वाला), and serves trustworthy wise people (आप्त उपसेवी च/बड़ों की सेवा करने वाला), remains free from disease (भवति अरोगः/निरोगी रहता है).
He who does not make efforts for unattainable things (अर्थेषु अलभ्येषु अकृत प्रयत्नम्/नामुमकिन चीजों में कोशिश न करना), but maintains respect daily for achievable means (कृत आदरम् नित्यम् उपायवत्सु/सही उपायों में ध्यान रखना).
And has mastered his senses (जितेन्द्रियम्/इंद्रियों को जीतने वाला); diseases do not make him repent (न अनुतपन्ति रोगाः/रोग परेशान नहीं करते), provided his destiny is not unfavorable at that time (यदि न अस्ति दैवम्/अगर भाग्य खराब न हो).
Favorable time (कालः अनुकूलः/अच्छा समय), pleasing sensory objects (विषयाः मनोज्ञाः/मनपसंद चीजें), righteous actions (धर्म्याः क्रियाः/अच्छे काम), and deeds that result in happiness (कर्म सुख अनुबन्धि/सुख देने वाले कार्य).
A controlled mind (सत्त्वम् विधेयम्/वश में रहने वाला मन), and a clear intellect (विशदा च बुद्धिः/साफ बुद्धि)—all these always lead to the happiness (सदा सुखाय/हमेशा सुख के लिए) of a wise courageous person (धीरस्य/धैर्यवान व्यक्ति के).
Thus ends the fifth chapter (पञ्चमः अध्यायः/पांचवां अध्याय) named 'Prevention of Diseases' (रोग अनुत्पादनीयः/रोगों को न पैदा होने देना).




Now, therefore, we shall explain the chapter (अध्याय/section) on the knowledge of liquid substances (द्रवद्रव्य/liquid materials).
Water gives life (जीवन/life giving), provides satiation (तर्पण/satisfaction), pleases the heart (हृद्य/heart pleasing), brings joy (ह्लादि/refreshing), and awakens the intellect (बुद्धिप्रबोधन/mind awakening).
It has an unmanifested taste (अव्यक्त रस/imperceptible taste), is clean, naturally cold (शीत/cooling), light to digest (लघु/light digestion), and is like nectar (अमृत/nectar-like).
Being exposed to sun and moon rays (सूर्य चंद्र/sun and moon), it becomes light (लघु/light) and destroys vata and kapha disorders (वात कफ हर/pacifier of vata and kapha).
Due to its coldness (शैत्य/coolness), life-giving nature (जीवन/vitality), and gentle quality, it overcomes pitta, blood disorders (रक्त विकार/blood disease), and poison agony (विष/toxin relief).
Pure rain water falling from the sky (गंगांबु/ganga rain water) is touched by sun, moon, and air (सूर्य चंद्र वायु/elements).
Its wholesome or unwholesome nature (हित अहित/beneficial or harmful) depends on the place and time (देश काल/region and season).
Rain water falling on clean white rice dish placed in silver vessel (राजत पात्र/silver vessel) without changing color is pure rainwater.
If it remains un-moistened and unchanged in color, it is Ganga rain water (गांग तोय/ganga rain water); otherwise it is different.
Ocean water (सामुद्र तोय/sea water) is not to be drunk except during the month of Ashwin (आश्वयुज/ashwin month September-October).
Water collected from washed rock surfaces, clean cloth filtration (वस्त्र पूत/cloth filtered water), or natural streams.
Water stored in gold, silver, or clay pots (हेम मृन्मय पात्र/gold and clay vessels) and undamaged should always be drunk.
In its absence, ground water (भूमिष्ठ जल/ground water) which mimics sky rain water qualities should be used.
Water on white soil is astringent-sweet (कषाय स्वादु/astringent sweet), on black soil is bitter (तिक्त/bitter), and on pale soil is yellowish.
On blue soil it is astringent-sweet (कषाय मधुर/astringent sweet), and in saline un-arable land it is salty (लवण/salty).
On brown soil it is alkaline (सक्षार/alkaline), on mixed soil it is mixed taste; when water quality dominates soil, it tastes sweet.
When soil qualities dominate, water becomes sweet, salty, or sour (स्वाद् लवण अम्ल/sweet salty sour) depending on dominant soil element.
When fire element dominates soil it is bitter-pungent (तिक्त कटु/bitter sharp), and when wind element dominates it is astringent (कषाय/astringent).
Ground water mimicking sky water with unmanifested taste (अव्यक्त रस/unmanifested taste) indicates dominant space element attributes.
Water from clean, expansive, dark or white regions exposed to sun and wind (सूर्य अनिल/sun and wind) is wholesome.
Water from wells (कौप/well water), lakes (सारस/lake water), ponds (ताडाग/pond water), hollows (चौंड्य/reservoir), waterfalls (प्रस्त्रवण/springs), and fountains (औद्भिद/fountain).
Stepwells (वापी/stepwell water) and river water (नदी तोय/river water)—thus ground water is categorized into eight types (अष्टधा/eightfold).
Well water is slightly alkaline (सक्षार/alkaline), increases pitta (पित्तकृत्/pitta increaser), kindles digestion (दीपन/appetite booster), and does not excessively aggravate vata.
Lake water is sweet (स्वादु/sweet) and light (लघु/light digestion); pond water is heavy (गुरु/heavy digestion) and aggravates vata (वातल/vata increaser).
Small reservoir water increases pitta (पित्तल/pitta increaser); waterfall spring water destroys all humor disorders (दोषहर/humor pacifier).
Natural fountain water is sweet (स्वादु/sweet) and conquers pitta (पित्तघ्न/pitta reducer); clean stepwell water is sweet and light (लघु/light digestion).
River water aggravates vata (वातल/vata increaser), is dry (रूक्ष/dry), and pungent in post-digestion (कटुक/pungent).
River water lightness or heaviness (गुरु लाघव/heavy or light) depends on proximity to desert, marshland, or mountains (धन्व अनूप महीध्र/desert marsh mountain).
Rivers flowing towards western ocean (पश्चिमोदधि/western sea), swift-flowing, and having clean water.
These western rivers are generally wholesome (पथ्य/wholesome); rivers flowing otherwise with opposite features are unwholesome.
Waters churning and splashing over stones, broken rocks, and churning currents (उपला स्फालन/rock splashing).
Waters originating from Himalaya and Malaya mountains (हिमवत् मलय/himalaya malaya mountains) are wholesome when flowing, but harmful when stagnant.
Stagnant mountain waters produce intestinal worms (कृमि/parasites), elephantiasis (श्लीपद/filariasis), throat diseases (कंठ रोग/throat ailments), and head ailments (शिरो रोग/headaches).
Rivers originating from Avanti, Aparanta, and Mahendra mountains cause hemorrhoids (दुर्नाम/piles).
Rivers originating from Sahya and Vindhya mountains produce abdominal diseases (उदर/ascites), elephantiasis (श्लीपद/filariasis), and heart ailments.
Rivers originating from Pariyatra mountain destroy all three humors (दोषघ्नी/tridosha pacifier), but cure skin diseases, anemia, and head ailments.
Rivers enhancing strength and virility (बल पौरुष/strength and stamina) are wholesome, whereas sea water aggravates all three humors (त्रिदोषकृत्/tridosha aggravator).
Water polluted by insects, snakes, urine, feces, putrefaction, grass, and fallen leaves (कीट अहि मूत्र/insects snakes urine).
Water covered with mud, lotus weeds, algae, and dense floating leaves (पंक शैवाल/mud and algae).
Water un-exposed to sun, moon, or wind (सूर्य चंद्र पवन/sun moon wind) and inhabited by tiny poisonous creatures.
Water newly fallen as rain, discolored, turbid, and possessing thick foam (कलुष फेनिल/turbid foamy).
Water with bad taste, unpleasant smell, thick density, and causing tooth sensitivity due to extreme chillness (अतिशैत्य/extreme coldness).
Water unseasonal or rain water falling in the very first unseasonal shower (अनार्त्तवं/unseasonal rain).
Water polluted by contact with spider webs, feces, urine, or insect venom (लूता विष/spider venom pollution).
Bathing in or drinking such polluted water causes thirst (तृष्णा/thirst), flatulence (आध्मान/bloating), abdominal enlargement, and fever (ज्वर/fever).
It also causes cough (कास/coughing), digestive suppression (अग्निसाद/suppressed digestion), eye inflammation (अभिष्यंद/conjunctivitis), itching (कंडू/itching), and neck swellings.
One should avoid such contaminated water; if no other water is available, purification is recommended (तोय प्रसादन/water purification).
Filtration through thick cloth (घनवस्त्र परिस्राव/thick cloth filtering) protects from tiny aquatic insects.
Purify defective water by heating it over fire, exposing to sun, or quenching red-hot iron balls (अग्नि अर्क/fire and sun heating).
Purify water by adding fragrant leaves, lotus roots, clearing nuts (कतक/clearing nut Kataka), or algae.
Or purify water by placing precious stones like diamond and topaz (वज्र गोमेद/diamond and topaz) to settle impurities.
Remove foul odor from water by infusing flowers like Patala and Karavira (पाटला करवीर पुष्प/patala karavira blossoms).
Water from another region should not be drunk immediately without getting accustomed (अन्य प्रदेश जल/different region water).
In indigestion, boiled water (कथित जल/boiled water) should be taken; in acute undigested state or when food is digested, raw unboiled water is prohibited.
This rule applies during cold seasons (शीत विधि/cold regimen); in indigestion, cold unboiled water must be strictly avoided.
Water is the very life of living beings (प्राण/vital life force) and the entire universe consists of water (तन्मय/water constituent).
Therefore, even when water intake is restricted in diseases, total prohibition of water is never advised (वारि न वार्यते/water never totally barred).
Complete denial of water causes severe thirst, tissue emaciation, organ exhaustion (अंगसाद/debility), or even death (मृत्यु/death).
Indeed, no living activity is possible without water for both healthy and diseased persons (स्वस्थ व्याधित/healthy and sick).
Pure water boiled with medicinal herbs (औषध संस्कृत/medicated boiled water), taken warm or cooled, is beneficial.
Water taken in proper measure after due consideration acts like nectar (अमृत/nectar); taken improperly it acts like poison (विष/poison).
Excessive drinking of water, even when thirsty, causes harm (अतियोग/excessive intake).
Excess water converts into pitta and kapha disorders, especially in feverish patients (ज्वरित/fever patient).
Excess water increases undigested toxins (आम/undigested toxin), thirst, sleepiness (तंद्रा/drowsiness), flatulence, and body heaviness (अंगगौरव/heaviness of limbs).
It aggravates cough (कास/coughing), digestive suppression (अग्निसाद/suppressed digestion), nausea (हृल्लास/nausea), excessive salivation, dyspnea, and chronic rhinitis.
Water boiled and cooled becomes sweet in digestion (स्वादु/sweet) and cold in potency (हिम/cooling), yet warm water is used appropriately.
Therefore, avoid improper water intake and consume water lightly according to digestive capacity (लाघव/lightness).
In conditions of undigested toxins and intestinal sluggishness (आम विष्टब्ध/ama and indigestion), lukewarm water (कोष्ण जल/lukewarm water) should be drunk even if not thirsty.
Drink only as much water as needed to moisten food, because excessive moisture destroys digestive fire (अग्निनाशन/destroyer of digestive fire).
Lukewarm water breaks kapha and vata blockages (कफ वात विबद्ध/kapha vata obstruction) and frees stomach stagnation.
Food liquefied with lukewarm water (कोष्ण तोय/warm water) gets digested rapidly (क्षिप्रं पच्यते/quick digestion).
For persons with unstable digestive fire or weakened by disease (व्याधि क्षीण/disease weakened).
Even a small quantity of unboiled raw water (आम उदक/unboiled water) is unwholesome, as it aggravates all three humors (त्रिदोषकृत्/tridosha aggravator).
Because water is opposite to fire element (तेजः प्रतिपक्ष/opposite to fire), persons with weak digestion (मंदाग्नि/sluggish digestion) should avoid raw water.
Similarly, patients suffering from abnormal fat (मेद/obesity), enlarged spleen (प्लीहा/splenomegaly), abscess (विद्रधि/abscess), and abdominal tumors (गुल्म/phantom tumor) should avoid raw water.
Patients with anemia (पांडु/anemia), dropsy/ascites (उदर/ascites), diarrhea (अतिसार/diarrhea), hemorrhoids (अर्श/piles), sprue (ग्रहणी/sprue), wasting (शोष/wasting), and swelling (शोफ/edema) should restrict water.
If unable to abstain due to thirst, they may drink small amounts of medicated water (औषध संस्कृत/medicated water).
Except in autumn and summer seasons (शरद् निदाघ/autumn and summer), even healthy persons should drink water in moderation (अल्पशः/moderate quantity).
Water drunk at the beginning of a meal (भक्त आदौ/before meal) suppresses digestive fire and causes emaciation (कृशांगता/body thinness).
Water drunk at the end of a meal (भक्त अंते/after meal) produces obesity (स्थूलत्व/corpulence) and increases kapha in stomach (उर्ध्व कफ/upper stomach kapha).
Water drunk in the middle of a meal (भक्त मध्ये/during meal) maintains medium build (मध्यांगता/medium physique), balances tissue elements (धातु साम्य/tissue equilibrium), and aids comfortable digestion.
Cold water overcomes intoxication (मदात्यय/alcoholism), exhaustion (ग्लानि/faintness), fainting (मूर्च्छा/syncope), vomiting (छर्दि/emesis), fatigue, and dizziness (भ्रम/giddiness).
Cold water destroys excessive thirst, body heat (उष्म/heat), burning sensation (दाह/burning feeling), bleeding pitta (पित्तास्र/bleeding pitta), and poison effects (विष/poisoning).
Water boiled until reduced by one-fourth or one-third (पाद त्रिभाग क्षीण/reduced by 1/4 or 1/3) according to season, region, and digestive heaviness.
Boiled water that is free from foam (फेनरहित/foam free), clean, and clear is wholesome.
Warm boiled water relieves hiccups (हिक्का/hiccups), flatulence (आध्मान/bloating), vata, kapha, thirst, cough, dyspnea, and chronic rhinitis.
It cures flank pain (पार्श्वशूल/flank pain), undigested toxins (आम/ama toxins), obesity (मेद/obesity), recent fever (नवज्वर/acute fever), and aids prompt detoxification.
Warm water kindles digestion (दीपन/appetite booster), digests toxins (पाचन/digestive), clears throat (कंठ्य/throat cleanser), is light (लघु/light digestion), and cleanses urinary bladder (बस्तिविशोधन/bladder cleanser).
Water heated by contact with hot stones, silver, clay pots, gold, or sun rays (अर्क तापित/sun heated).
Such heated water, drunk warm or cooled, pacifies all three humors (त्रिदोषघ्न/tridosha pacifier) and conquers severe thirst.
Boiled water allowed to cool naturally (कथित शीतल/boiled cooled water) is light (लघु/light), dry (रूक्ष/dry), kills worms (कृमिघ्न/parasite killer), and pacifies thirst.
Boiled cooled water is commended in combined pitta-kapha aggravation (पित्तकफ संसर्ग/pitta kapha disorder) and acute tridosha fever (सन्निपात/tridosha fever).
Water boiled and kept overnight loses its fire attributes (वह्निगुण भ्रष्ट/devoid of fire qualities), turns sour in digestion (अम्ल पाक/sour post-digestion), and aggravates all humors.
Stale water kept overnight (पर्युषित जल/stale water) behaves similarly; water formed from hail (करकोद्भव जल/hailstone water) is heavy.
Hailstone water due to extreme coldness, heaviness, and density aggravates kapha and vata (कफवातकृत्/kapha and vata aggravator).
Water exposed to moonstone rays (चंद्रकांतभव/moonstone water) cures demonic affliction, poisons, pitta disorders, and fever (पित्तज्वर/pitta fever).
It improves eyesight (दृष्टि/vision), intellect (मेधा/intelligence), body stability (वपुः स्थैर्य/physique stability), is sweet (स्वादु/sweet), cold, and light.
Coconut water (नारिकेल उदक/tender coconut water) is unctuous (स्निग्ध/unctuous), sweet (स्वादु/sweet), aphrodisiac (वृष्य/virility booster), cold, and light (लघु/light digestion).
It relieves thirst, pitta humor, and vata wind (तृष्णा पित्त अनिल/thirst pitta vata), kindles appetite (दीपन/digestion booster), and cleanses bladder (बस्ति शोधन/urinary tract cleanser).
In rainy season (वर्षा ऋतु/monsoon season), sky rain water is best (वर/superior) and river water is inferior (अवर/inferior quality).
Thus ends the classification of waters (तोयवर्ग/water group).
Cow milk (गव्य/cow milk), buffalo milk (माहिष/buffalo milk), goat milk (आज/goat milk), camel milk (कारभ/camel milk), human breast milk (त्रैण/human milk), and sheep milk (आविकी/sheep milk).
Elephant milk (ऐभ/elephant milk) and single-hoofed animal milk like horse/donkey (ऐकशफ/one-hoofed animal milk)—thus milk is remembered as eightfold (अष्टविध/eight types).
Milk is sweet in taste and digestion (स्वादु/sweet taste), unctuous (स्निग्ध/oily), enhances vital essence (ओजस्य/immunity booster), and increases tissue elements (धातु वर्द्धन/tissue nourishing).
It alleviates vata and pitta humors (वात पित्त हर/pacifier of vata and pitta), acts as an aphrodisiac (वृष्य/virility booster), increases kapha (श्लेष्मल/kapha increaser), heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy), and cold in potency (शीतल/cooling).
Generally, cow milk (गव्य पय/cow milk) is supreme here; it promotes life (जीवनीय/vitalizer) and acts as a rejuvenator (रसायन/rejuvenating tonic).
It is wholesome for emaciated and wounded persons (क्षत क्षीण/injured and weak), promotes intellect (मेध्य/brain booster), improves skin complexion (वर्ण्य/skin glow), increases breast milk (स्तन्यकर/lactation promoter), and is laxative (सर/mild laxative).
It destroys fatigue (श्रम/exhaustion), dizziness (भ्रम/giddiness), intoxication (मद/drunkenness), misfortune, dyspnea (श्वास/breathing difficulty), cough (कास/coughing), severe thirst (तृट्/excessive thirst), and hunger (क्षुधा/appetite).
It cures chronic fever (जीर्ण ज्वर/old fever), painful urination (मूत्रकृच्छ्र/dysuria), and bleeding disorders (रक्तपित्त/bleeding disease).
Buffalo milk (माहिष/buffalo milk) is cold, very heavy (गरीयः/heavy digestion), wholesome for persons with hyperactive digestion (अत्यग्नि/hyperactive digestion) and insomnia (अनिद्रा/sleeplessness).
Goat milk (आज/goat milk) is light to digest (लघु/easy to digest) because goats drink little water, exercise much, and eat pungent and bitter plants (कटु तिक्त/bitter plants).
Goat milk overcomes wasting disease/tuberculosis (शोष/emaciation), fever (ज्वर/fever), breathlessness (श्वास/dyspnea), bleeding disorders (रक्तपित्त/bleeding disease), and diarrhea (अतिसार/loose motions).
Camel milk (औष्ट्रक/camel milk) is slightly dry (ईषत् रूक्ष/slightly dry), hot in potency (उष्ण/heating), salty in taste (लवण/salty), kindles digestive fire (दीपन/appetite booster), and is light to digest (लघु/light digestion).
It is commendable for vata-kapha disorders (वात कफ/wind phlegm), abdominal distension (आनाह/bloating), intestinal worms (कृमि/parasites), edema (शोफ/swelling), enlargement of abdomen (उदर/ascites), and hemorrhoids (अर्श/piles).
Human breast milk (मानुष/woman breast milk) overcomes vata-pitta disorders (वात पित्त/wind bile), blood ailments (रक्त/blood diseases), traumatic injury (अभिघात/physical trauma), and eye diseases (अक्षिरोग/eye afflictions).
Used through gratifying drops (तर्पण/soothing drop), eye drops (आश्चोतन/eye instillation), and nasal drops (नस्य/nasal drops); sheep milk (आविकी/sheep milk) is unpleasing to heart (अहृद्य/unpalatable) and hot in potency (उष्ण/heating).
Sheep milk destroys vata diseases (वातव्याधि/neurological disorder), but causes hiccup (हिक्का/hiccups), dyspnea (श्वास/breathlessness), pitta and kapha aggravation (पित्त कफ प्रद/pitta kapha increaser).
Elephant milk (हस्तिनी/elephant milk) brings firmness to body (स्थैर्यकृत्/body stabilizer), is very heavy (गाढ/dense heavy), hot (उष्ण/heating); horse/single-hoof milk (ऐकशफ/one-hoofed milk) is light (लघु/light).
Single-hoof milk cures peripheral vata (शाखावात/gout joint pain), is sour-salty (अम्ल लवण/sour salty), and causes dullness/sluggishness (जडताकर/stupor).
Raw milk (आम/raw milk) is channel-blocking (अभिष्यंदि/channel clogging) and heavy (गुरु/heavy); appropriately boiled milk (शृत/boiled milk) has opposite healthy properties (विपरीत/wholesome).
Except for woman breast milk (वनितास्तन्य/human milk), which is beneficial when taken raw/fresh (आम/raw milk).
Over-boiled milk (अतिशृत/excessively boiled) becomes very heavy (गरीयः/heavy); fresh warm milk directly from udder (धारोष्ण/warm drawn milk) is like nectar (अमृत/nectar-like).
Milk from cows eating oilcake or sour foods (पिण्याक अम्ल/oilcake sour diet) becomes heavy (गुरु/heavy) and channel-blocking (अभिष्यंदि/channel clogging).
Lack of exercise (अचेष्टा/physical inactivity) and evening milking (प्रादोष/evening time) make milk heavier, acting like medicine (औषध/therapeutic dose).
Conversely, physical activity (चेष्टा/exercise) and natural habits (प्रकृति/natural disposition) bring lightness (लघिमा/lightness) to milk.
Milk from small-bodied animals (ह्रस्व/smaller beasts) is lighter than from huge-bodied animals; the same rule applies to meats (मांस/animal flesh).
Yogurt/curd (दधि/curd) is sour in taste and digestion (अम्ल/sour), astringent (ग्राहि/bowel binder), heavy (गुरु/heavy), hot in potency (उष्ण/heating), and pacifies vata (वातजित्/vata reducer).
It increases fat tissue (मेद/fat tissue), semen (शुक्र/semen), strength (बल/stamina), kapha, blood, pitta, digestive fire (अग्नि/digestion), and swelling (शोफ/edema).
It gives appetite (रोचिष्णु/taste enhancer), commendable in anorexia (अरुचि/loss of appetite), cold fever (शीतक/chilling fever), and irregular fever (विषमज्वर/malaria fever).
Useful in chronic rhinitis (पीनस/catarrh), dysuria (मूत्रकृच्छ्र/painful urination); churned dry curd without fat (रूक्ष/fatless curd) is good in sprue/bowel disorder (ग्रहणीगद/IBS sprue).
One should never eat curd at night (निशि/night time), nor heated (उष्ण/hot curd), nor in spring, summer, and autumn seasons (वसन्त उष्ण शरद्/spring summer autumn).
Do not consume curd without green-gram soup (मुद्गसूप/mung soup), honey (क्षौद्र/honey), ghee (घृत/ghee), or sugar candy (सितोपल/rock sugar).
Do not eat curd without Indian gooseberry (आमलक/amla), nor un-instantiated/unformed curd (मंद/unformed curd), nor daily (नित्य/daily consumption).
Wrong consumption of curd causes fever (ज्वर/fever), bleeding pitta (रक्तपित्त/hemorrhage), erysipelas (वीसर्प/skin disease), skin lesions/leprosy (कुष्ठ/skin disorders), anemia (पांडु/anemia), and dizziness (भ्रम/giddiness).
Buttermilk (तक्र/buttermilk) is light (लघु/light digestion), astringent-sour (कषाय अम्ल/astringent sour), kindles digestion (दीपन/appetite booster), and overcomes kapha and vata (कफ वात जित्/kapha vata pacifier).
It cures edema (शोफ/swelling), enlargement of abdomen (उदर/ascites), hemorrhoids (अर्श/piles), sprue (ग्रहणी/irritable bowel), dysuria (मूत्रग्रह/urinary retention), and anorexia (अरुचि/loss of appetite).
It overcomes abdominal tumor (गुल्म/phantom tumor), enlarged spleen (प्लीहा/spleen disease), ghee complications (घृत व्यापद्/ghee indigestion), slow poison (गर/toxic poison), and anemia (पांडु/anemia).
Likewise, buttermilk whey (मस्तु/curd whey) is laxative (सर/laxative), cleanses body channels (स्रोतो शोधि/channel cleanser), relieves constipation/flatulence (विष्टंभजित्/bloating reliever), and is light (लघु/light).
Freshly churned butter (नवनीत/fresh butter) is cold (शीत/cooling), sweet (स्वादु/sweet), astringent-sour (कषाय अम्ल/astringent sour).
It overcomes tuberculosis (यक्ष्मा/consumption), hemorrhoids (अर्श/piles), facial paralysis (अर्धित/facial palsy), bleeding pitta (रक्तपित्त/bleeding disease), vata, is bowel-binding (ग्राहि/astringent), and kindles digestion (दीपन/digestion enhancer).
Butter extracted directly from milk (क्षीरोद्भव नवनीत/milk-extracted butter) is astringent (संग्राहि/bowel binder) and cures bleeding disorders (रक्तपित्त/bleeding disease) and eye afflictions (अक्षिरोग/eye diseases).
Clarified butter/Ghee (घृत/ghee) is commendable for intellect (धी/wisdom), memory (स्मृति/memory retention), intelligence (मेधा/cognition), digestion fire (अग्नि/digestive fire), strength (बल/vitality), lifespan (आयु/longevity), semen (शुक्र/reproductive tissue), and eyesight (चक्षु/vision).
Good for children (बाल/children), elderly (वृद्ध/elderly), those desiring offspring (प्रजा/progeny seeker), beauty (कांति/radiance), softness of body (सौकुमार्य/delicacy), and clear voice (स्वर/voice tone).
Helpful for sufferers of physical wounds (क्षत/trauma), wasting (क्षीण/debility), erysipelas (परीसर्प/spreading lesions), and weapon or fire burns (शस्त्र अग्नि/burns and cut injuries).
Alleviates vata, pitta, poison (विष/toxins), insanity (उन्माद/psychosis), edema (शोफ/swelling), ill-fortune (अलक्ष्मी/misfortune), and fever (ज्वर/pyrexia).
Ghee is the best among unctuous substances (स्नेह उत्तम/supreme healthy fat), cold in potency (शीत/cooling), and supreme anti-aging agent (वयः स्थापन/rejuvenator).
Processed with a thousand methods (सहस्र वीर्य/thousand power methods), ghee performs a thousand therapeutic actions (कर्म सहस्रकृत्/thousand healing functions).
Old aged ghee (पुराण घृत/aged ghee) conquers intoxication (मद/drunkenness), epilepsy (अपस्मार/fits), fainting (मूर्च्छा/syncope), head, ear, eye, and uterine diseases (शिरः कर्ण अक्षि योनि/head ear eye womb ailments).
Aged ghee cleanses wounds (व्रण शोधन/wound cleanser) and heals wounds (व्रण रोपण/wound healer).
It possesses all earlier mentioned qualities in far greater measure, acting like nectar (अमृत/nectar-like elixir).
Similarly, ghee scum/supernatant layer (घृतमंड/ghee scum) is dry (रूक्ष/dry), sharp/penetrating (तीक्ष्ण/penetrating), and light/thin (तनु/thin density).
Cheese/curd preparations like Kilata (किलाट/dense curd cheese), Kuchika (कूचिका/boiled curd whey cheese), Takrapindaka (तक्रपिंडक/buttermilk cake), Morana (मोरण/fermented curd) give strength (बल्य/strength booster).
Preparations with boiled milk/colostrum (पीयूष/first milk colostrum), plant extracts, and curd solids reduce digestive fire (वह्नि सादन/digestion suppressor).
They produce semen (शुक्र/semen), sleep (निद्रा/sleep), kapha (कफ/phlegm), cause constipation/flatulence (विष्टंभी/causing bloating), heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy), and aggravate all humors (दोषल/humor aggravator).
Understand the good and bad properties of curd, ghee, etc., according to the milk source (पयः/milk origin).
Cow milk and cow ghee (गव्य क्षीर घृत/cow milk and ghee) are best (श्रेष्ठ/supreme), while sheep milk products (आविसंभव/sheep milk origin) are condemned (निंदित/inferior).
Thus ends the classification of milk and milk products (क्षीरवर्ग/milk group).
Sugarcane juice (इक्षुरस/sugarcane juice) is heavy (गुरु/heavy digestion), unctuous (स्निग्ध/oily), nourishing (बृंहण/body builder), increases kapha and urine (कफ मूत्रकृत्/kapha and urine increaser).
Aphrodisiac (वृष्य/virility booster), cold (शीत/cooling), pacifies vata (पवनजित्/vata reducer), but taken after meals it aggravates vata (वात प्रकोप/vata aggravation).
Relieves bleeding disorders (रक्तपित्त/bleeding disease), sweet in taste and digestion (स्वादु/sweet taste), and laxative (सर/mild laxative).
The tip of sugarcane (अग्रे/tip portion) is slightly salty (सलवण/slightly salty); chewing with teeth (दंतपीडित/chewed with teeth) makes juice pure like sugar (शर्करासम/sugar-like purity).
Machine-extracted sugarcane juice (यांत्रिक रस/machine crushed juice) mixed with roots, tips, bugs, dirt, and stored for some time undergoes deterioration (विकृति/quality alteration).
Machine-pressed juice causes burning sensation (विदाही/causing burning), is heavy (गुरु/heavy), causes constipation/flatulence (विष्टंभी/bloating); among types, Paundraka sugarcane (पौंड्रक/paundraka cane) is superior.
Paundraka sugarcane juice is best due to coldness (शैत्य/coolness), clarity (प्रसाद/transparency), and sweetness (माधुर्य/sweetness); next to it is Vamshika sugarcane (वांशिक/vamshika cane).
Next in order are Shataparvaka (शतपर्वक), Kantara (कांतार), Naipala (नैपाल) sugarcane varieties.
These lower varieties are slightly alkaline (सक्षार/slightly alkaline), astringent (सकषाय/slightly astringent), warm (सोष्ण/heating), and cause mild burning sensation (विदाही/mild burning).
Molasses/Fanita (फाणित/half-cooked jaggery syrup) is heavy (गुरु/heavy), channel-blocking (अभिष्यंदि/channel clogging), increases humors (चयकृत्/humor aggregator), and cleanses urine (मूत्र शोधन/urinary tract cleanser).
Molasses/Fanita is heavy (गुरु/heavy), channel-blocking (अभिष्यंदि/channel clogging), increases humors (चयकृत्/humor aggregator), and cleanses urine (मूत्र शोधन/urinary tract cleanser).
Washing-processed pure jaggery/Gur (धौत गुड़/washed jaggery) does not excessively increase kapha (न अति श्लेष्मकर/mild kapha increaser), facilitates easy passage of urine and feces (सृष्ट मूत्र शकृत्/clearing urine and stool).
Unwashed crude jaggery (अपर गुड़/unwashed crude jaggery) produces abundance of worms (कृमि/parasites), bone marrow (मज्जा/marrow), blood (असृक्/blood), fat (मेद/fat), muscle (मांस/flesh), and kapha (कफ/phlegm).
Old jaggery (पुराण गुड़/aged jaggery) is pleasing to heart (हृद्य/heart pleasing) and wholesome (पथ्य/wholesome); new fresh jaggery (नव गुड़/fresh jaggery) increases kapha and suppresses digestion (श्लेष्माग्निसादकृत्/kapha increaser digestion suppressor).
Sugar products increase virility (वृष्य/virility booster), wholesome for injured and emaciated (क्षीण क्षत हित/good for weak and injured), overcome bleeding pitta and vata (रक्तपित्त अनिल/bleeding pitta and vata pacifier).
Matsyandika (मत्स्यंडिका/raw sugar crystals), Khanda (खंड/unrefined sugar), and Sita (सिता/white refined sugar/rock sugar) are progressively superior in qualities (गुणवत्तर/higher quality).
Sugar made from bitters/herbs (तिक्त शर्करा/herbal sugar) is bitter-sweet (तिक्त मधुर/bitter sweet), astringent (संकषाय/astringent), cold (हिम/cooling), and light (लघु/light digestion).
Refined sugar made from Kashepu/Darbha grasses (दर्भ शर्करा/darbha grass sugar) pacifies all three humors (त्रिदोषघ्नी/tridosha pacifier).
All sugars relieve burning sensation (दाह/burning sensation), thirst (तृट्/thirst), vomiting (छर्दि/emesis), fainting (मूर्च्छा/syncope), bleeding pitta (रक्तपित्त/bleeding disease), and are pleasing to heart (हृद्य/heart pleasing).
Among sugarcane products (इक्षु विकार/sugarcane derivatives), refined sugar (शर्करा/refined sugar) is the best (वर/superior) and half-cooked molasses syrup (फाणित/syrup) is the worst (अवर/inferior).
Thus ends the classification of sugarcane and sugar products (इक्षुवर्ग/sugarcane group).
Honey (मधु/honey) is good for eyes (चक्षुष्य/vision enhancer), scraping/cleansing agent (छेदि/scraping agent), relieves thirst (तृट्/thirst), kapha (श्लेष्म/phlegm), poisons (विष/toxins), hiccup (हिक्का/hiccups), and bleeding pitta (अस्रपित्त/bleeding disease).
Honey overcomes skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin disorders), diabetes/urinary disorders (मेह/diabetes), worms (कृमि/parasites), vomiting (छर्दि/emesis), dyspnea (श्वास/breathlessness), cough (कास/coughing), and diarrhea (अतिसार/loose motions).
Honey cleanses wounds (व्रण शोधन/wound cleanser), unites broken tissues (संधान/tissue joiner), heals wounds (रोपण/wound healer), and increases vata (वातल/vata increaser).
Honey is sweet in taste (मधुर/sweet taste), but pungent in digestion (कटुक पाके/pungent post-digestive effect); honey sugar (मधुशर्करा/honey sugar) possesses similar qualities.
Heated honey (उष्ण मधु/heated honey), honey taken by a heat-afflicted person (उष्णार्त्त/heat suffering person), in hot season (उष्णे/hot weather), or with hot foods (उष्णैः/hot combinations) kills a person (निहंति/fatal toxicity).
Because honey originates from poisonous flowers (विषपुष्प/poisonous flora) gathered by poisonous bees (सविष भ्रमर/poisonous bees).
Because honeybees themselves gather nectar from toxic flora with their venomous bodies.
Except in therapeutic emesis (प्रच्छर्दन/vomiting therapy) and non-unctuous enema (निरूह बस्ति/decoction enema), where warm honey is permitted (न निवार्यते/allowed).
Because in emesis and enema therapies, honey is quickly expelled out before getting digested (अलब्धपाक/un-absorbed elimination).
Due to being heavy (गुरु/heavy), dry (रूक्ष/dry), astringent (कषाय/astringent), and cold (शैत्य/cooling), honey is wholesome only in small quantity (अल्प हित/beneficial in small dose).
Indigestion caused by honey (मधु अजीर्ण/honey indigestion) is most terrible, because opposing treatments cannot be applied.
It causes immediate death like poison due to irreconcilable treatment complications (सद्यः हन्यात् विषं/fatal like poison).
Being derived from numerous plants (नानाद्रव्य/multi-plant origin), honey is a supreme synergist/catalyst (योगवाहि/synergistic carrier).
Combined with aphrodisiac remedies (वृष्ययोग/virility formulations), honey enhances aphrodisiac power (वृषता/virility boosting).
Among honey types: Makshika (माक्षिक/honey bee honey), Kshaudra (क्षौद्र/small bee honey), Pauttika (पौत्तिक/stinging bee honey), and Bhramara (भ्रामर/giant bee honey) are progressively superior (यथोत्तर वर/progressively better).
Aged honey (जीर्ण मधु/stored aged honey) is superior among these; only the last two types (Makshika and Kshaudra) should be used regularly.
Thus ends the classification of honey (मधुवर्ग/honey group).
Oil (तैल/oil) resembles its source seed (स्वयोनि/source seed); among oils, sesame oil (तिलतैल/sesame oil) is main, sharp/penetrating (तीक्ष्ण/penetrating), fast-spreading (व्यवायि/pervading).
Excessive use causes skin diseases (त्वग्दोष/skin disorders), bad for eyes (अचक्षुष्य/harmful to vision), subtle (सूक्ष्म/subtle channel entering), hot (उष्ण/heating), does not increase kapha (कफ न कृत्/non kapha aggravator).
Nourishes emaciated persons (कृश बृंहण/weight gainer for thin) and reduces obese persons (स्थूल कर्शन/fat reducer for obese).
Binds stool (बद्धविट्क/stool binder), kills worms (कृमिघ्न/parasite destroyer), and processed with herbs it conquers all diseases (सर्व रोग जित्/universal disease conqueror).
By regular use of sesame oil, ancient demon lords (दैत्याधिपति/demon kings) became ageless (अजरा/ageless), free from disease (निर्विकार/disease free), overcame fatigue (जितश्रम/fatigue conqueror).
And possessed immense strength in battle against gods in ancient times.
Castor oil (एरंड तैल/castor oil) is bitter-pungent (तिक्त ओषण/bitter pungent), sweet (स्वादु/sweet), laxative (सर/laxative), and heavy (गुरु/heavy digestion).
Castor oil cures hernia/scrotal swelling (वर्ध्म/hernia), abdominal tumors (गुल्म/phantom tumor), vata and kapha disorders (अनिल कफ/vata and kapha), abdominal disease (उदर/ascites), and irregular fever (विषमज्वर/intermittent fever).
Relieves pain and swelling (रुक् शोफ/pain and swelling) located in waist (कटी/lumbar), groin (गुह्य/pelvic), abdomen (कोष्ठ/viscera), and back (पृष्ठ/spine).
Red castor oil (रक्त एरंड तैल/red castor oil) is penetrating (तीक्ष्ण/sharp), hot (उष्ण/heating), slimy (पिच्छिल/viscous), and strong-smelling (विस्त्र/foul smelling).
Mustard oil (सार्षप तैल/mustard oil) is pungent (कटु/pungent), hot (उष्ण/heating), sharp (तीक्ष्ण/penetrating), destroys kapha, semen, and vata (कफ शुक्र अनिल पह/kapha semen vata reducer).
Linseed oil (उमा/flaxseed oil) and Safflower oil (कुसुंभ/safflower oil) are hot in potency (सोष्ण/heating), cause skin diseases, kapha, and pitta (त्वग्दोष कफ पित्त कृत्/skin kapha pitta aggravator).
Oils derived from Danti root, Mulaka, Neem, Karanja, Arishta, and Shigru seeds.
Suvarchala, Ingudi, Pilu, Shankhini, and Pasambhava seeds.
Sarala pine, Aguru, Devadaru, Shimshapa wood extracts.
Tuvaraka and Bhallataka seeds are sharp (तीक्ष्ण/sharp), pungent (कटु/pungent), dry (रूक्ष/dry), and increase pitta (पित्तकृत्/pitta aggravator).
They destroy hemorrhoids (अर्श/piles), skin diseases/leprosy (कुष्ठ/skin diseases), worms (कृमि/parasites), kapha, semen, fat, and vata (शुक्र मेद अनिल/semen fat vata).
Karanja oil (करंज तैल/karanja oil) and Neem oil (निंब तैल/neem oil) are bitter (तिक्त/bitter) and not excessively hot (न अति उष्ण/mildly heating).
Pine resin oil/Sarala oil (सारल तैल/pine resin oil) is astringent-bitter-pungent (कषाय तिक्त कटुक/astringent bitter pungent) and cleanses wounds (व्रण शोधन/wound cleanser).
Tuvaraka oil (तौवरक तैल/chaulmoogra oil) and Bhallataka oil (अरुष्कर तैल/marking nut oil) are extremely hot (भृश उष्ण/intensely hot), sharp, and pungent.
They especially destroy worms and leprosy (कृमि कुष्ठ घ्न/worms and leprosy killer), and act as purgatives/emetics (उर्ध्व अधो विरेचन/upper and lower evacuants).
Oils derived from Aksha, Atimuktaka, Walnut, Coconut, and Madhuka seeds.
Cucumber seeds, Ervaruka, Pumpkin seeds, Shleshmataka, and Priyala seeds.
They pacify vata and pitta (वात पित्त हर/vata pitta pacifier), promote hair growth (केश्य/hair tonic), increase kapha (श्लेष्मल/kapha increaser), heavy (गुरु/heavy), and cold in potency (शीतल/cooling).
Oils from Gambhari (श्रीपर्णी) and Palash (किंशुक) seeds pacify pitta and kapha (पित्त श्लेष्म प्रशमन/pitta kapha pacifier).
Among all oils, Sesame oil (तिलतैल/sesame oil) is best (वर/superior), and Safflower oil (कौसुंभ/safflower oil) is worst (अवर/inferior).
Muscle fat (वसा/muscle fat) and Bone marrow (मज्जा/bone marrow) destroy vata (वातघ्न/vata pacifier), increase strength, pitta, and kapha (बल पित्त कफ प्रद/strength pitta kapha increaser).
Muscle fat and marrow possess similar properties as the flesh of the respective animal source (मांस अनुग/resembling source animal meat); fat tissue (मेद/adipose fat) also behaves similarly.
Among animal fats (वसा/muscle fat), fat of Owl (उलूकी), Pig (शौकरी), Swan (हंस), and Hen/Rooster (कुक्कुट) is superior.
Fat of Crocodile (कुंभीर) and Buffalo (महिष) is also specific.
Fat of Crow (काक), Water-crow (मद्गु), and Teal/Duck (कारंडव) is condemned/inferior (निंदित/inferior).
Among leaf-eating animal fats, Goat fat (छाग मेद/goat fat) is best (वर/superior) and Elephant fat (हास्तिन मेद/elephant fat) is worst (अवर/inferior).
Thus ends the classification of oils and animal fats (तैलवर्ग/oil group).
Wine (मद्य/alcoholic beverages) kindles digestive fire (दीपन/appetite booster), enhances taste (रोचन/flavor enhancer), sharp/penetrating (तीक्ष्ण/penetrating), hot in potency (उष्ण/heating), provides satisfaction and nourishment (तुष्टि पुष्टि प्रद/contentment and stamina provider).
Sweet, bitter, pungent in taste (स्वाद् तिक्त कटुक/sweet bitter pungent), sour in post-digestive effect (अम्ल पाक/sour post-digestion), and laxative (सर/laxative).
Slightly astringent (सकषाय/slightly astringent), improves voice (स्वर/voice tone), health (आरोग्य/wellness), intellect (प्रतिभा/intellectual clarity), skin complexion (वर्ण/skin glow), and light to digest (लघु/light digestion).
Wholesome for those with insomnia or excessive sleep (नष्ट निद्रा अति निद्रा/sleeplessness or oversleeping), but vitiates pitta and blood (पित्त अस्र दूषण/pitta and blood aggravator).
Useful for thin and obese persons (कृश स्थूल हित/beneficial for lean and heavy), dry (रूक्ष/dry), subtle (सूक्ष्म/channel penetrating), cleanses body channels (स्रोतो विशोधन/channel cleanser).
Destroys vata and kapha when drunk properly (युक्त्या पीत/consumed properly); taken improperly, it acts like poison (विषवत्/like toxin).
Fresh new wine (नव मद्य/new wine) is heavy (गुरु/heavy) and aggravates humors (दोष जनन/humor aggravator); aged wine (जीर्ण मद्य/aged wine) possesses opposite healthy qualities (विपरीत/wholesome).
Wine should not be drunk with hot treatments (उष्ण उपचार/hot regimen), nor by persons after purgation (विरिक्त/after bowel evacuation), nor when afflicted with hunger (क्षुधा आतुर/hungry person).
Avoid wine that is excessively strong, very mild, cloudy, thick, or spoiled/deteriorated (व्यापन्न/spoiled wine).
Sura wine (सुरा/fermented grain wine) relieves abdominal tumor (गुल्म/phantom tumor), enlargement of abdomen (उदर/ascites), hemorrhoids (अर्श/piles), sprue (ग्रहणी/irritable bowel), emaciating disease (शोष/wasting), is unctuous (स्नेहनी/unctuous), and heavy (गुरु/heavy).
Sura pacifies vata (अनिलघ्नी/vata pacifier), increases fat (मेद/fat), blood (अस्र/blood), breast milk (स्तन्य/lactation), urine (मूत्र/urine), and kapha (कफ/phlegm).
Varuni wine (वारुणी/date-palm or coconut wine) has similar properties, pleasing to heart (हृद्या/heart pleasing), light (लघु/light), sharp (तीक्ष्ण/penetrating), and destroys pain/colic (शूल/colic pain).
Varuni cures cough (कास/coughing), vomiting (वमि/emesis), dyspnea (श्वास/breathlessness), constipation (विबंध/constipation), abdominal distension (आध्मान/bloating), and chronic rhinitis (पीनस/catarrh).
Beneficial in pain (शूल/pain), dysentery (प्रवाहिका/tenesmus), edema (शोफ/swelling), thirst (तृष्णा/thirst), rumbling abdomen (आटोप/flatulence), and hemorrhoids (अर्श/piles).
Jagala wine (जंगल/wine dregs liquor) is digestive (पाचन/digestive), bowel binder (ग्राही/astringent), dry (रूक्ष/dry), and Medaka wine (मेदक/fermented dregs) behaves similarly.
Bakkasa wine (बक्कस/leftover grain residue wine) is depleted of essence (हृतसार/devoid of nutrients), causes constipation/flatulence (विष्टंभी/causing bloating), and aggravates humors (दोष कोपन/humor aggravator).
Vibhitaki Sura (बैभीतकी सुरा/belleric myrobalan wine) does not cause intense intoxication (न अति तीव्र मद/mildly intoxicating), is light (लघु/light), and wholesome (पथ्य/wholesome).
Not strongly contraindicated in wounds (व्रण/wounds), anemia (पांडू/anemia), and skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin disorders).
Yava Sura/Barley wine (यवसुरा/barley beer) causes flatulence (विष्टंभी/bloating), heavy (गुरु/heavy), dry (रूक्ष/dry), and aggravates all three humors (त्रिदोषला/tridosha aggravator).
Kohala wine (कौहली/barley malt wine) is nourishing (बृंहणी/body builder) and heavy; Madhulaka (मधूलक/sweet grain wine) increases kapha (श्लेष्मल/kapha increaser).
Arishta/Fermented herbal decoction (अरिष्ट/herbal fermented decoction) possesses properties of its constituent ingredients (यथाद्रव्य/as per herbs) and excels all wines in virtues (सर्व मद्य गुणाधिक/superior to all wines).
Cures sprue (ग्रहणी/sprue), anemia (पांडु/anemia), skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin disorders), hemorrhoids (अर्श/piles), emaciation (शोष/wasting), edema (शोफ/swelling), abdominal disease (उदर/ascites), and fever (ज्वर/fever).
Destroys abdominal tumors (गुल्म/phantom tumor), intestinal worms (कृमि/parasites), enlarged spleen (प्लीहा/spleen enlargement), is astringent-pungent (कषाय कटु/astringent pungent), and increases vata (वातल/vata increaser).
Mardvika/Grape wine (मार्द्दीक/grape wine) is scraping agent (लेखन/scraping agent), pleasing to heart (हृद्य/heart pleasing), not excessively hot (न अति उष्ण/mildly heating), sweet (मधुर/sweet), and laxative (सर/laxative).
Mildly affects pitta and vata (अल्प पित्त अनिल/mild pitta vata impact), cures anemia (पांडु/anemia), diabetes/urinary disorders (मेह/diabetes), hemorrhoids (अर्श/piles), and worms (कृमि/parasites).
Kharjura/Date wine (खार्जूर/date palm wine) has slightly lesser qualities than grape wine, increases vata (वातल/vata increaser), and is heavy (गुरु/heavy).
Sharkara/Sugar wine (शार्कर/sugar wine) is fragrant (सुरभि/aromatic), sweet (स्वादु/sweet), pleasing to heart (हृद्य/heart pleasing), not excessively intoxicating (न अति मद/mildly intoxicating), and light (लघु/light).
Gaudika/Jaggery wine (गौड/jaggery wine) clears urine, feces, and gas (सृष्ट मूत्र शकृत् वात/evacuator), provides gratification (तर्पण/satiation), and kindles digestion (दीपन/digestion enhancer).
Sidhu/Sugarcane juice wine (शीधु/boiled sugarcane juice wine) increases vata and pitta (वात पित्त कर/vata pitta increaser), destroys unctuousness and kapha disorders (स्नेह श्लेष्म विकार हा/kapha and fat reducer).
Reduces fat (मेद/fat), edema (शोफ/swelling), abdominal disease (उदर/ascites), hemorrhoids (अर्श/piles); boiled juice Sidhu (पक्वरस शीधु/boiled sugarcane wine) is superior (वर/superior).
Madhvasava/Honey wine (मध्वासव/fermented honey wine) is scraping (छेदी/scraping agent) and penetrating (तीक्ष्ण/sharp).
It overcomes diabetes (मेह/diabetes), chronic rhinitis (पीनस/catarrh), and cough (कास/coughing).
Surasava/Distilled liquor (सुरासव/distilled grain spirit) has sharp intoxication (तीक्ष्ण मद/intense intoxication), sweet-sharp taste, and pacifies vata (अनिलापह/vata pacifier).
Maireya/Compounded sweet wine (मैरेय/spiced molasses wine) is sweet (मधुर/sweet), aphrodisiac (वृष्य/virility booster), laxative (सर/laxative), gratifying (संतर्पण/nourishing), and heavy (गुरु/heavy).
Dhataki-fermented wine (धातक्याभिशुत/dhataki flower wine) when aged is dry (रूक्ष/dry), improves taste (रोचन/appetizing), and kindles digestion (दीपन/digestion booster).
Draksasava/Raisin wine (द्राक्षासव/fermented raisin elixir) is similar to honey, and excels as a digestion kindle booster (परम दीपन/supreme digestion enhancer).
Wine from raisins and sugarcane juice (मृद्वीका इक्षुरस आसव/raisin sugarcane juice wine) is described as similar to grape wine.
Generally, Asava (आसव/fermented wine infusion) is pleasing to heart (हृद्य/heart pleasing), increases vata (वातल/vata increaser), and inherits the properties of its component drugs (स्वऔषधानुग/drug properties).
Raisins, sugarcane, honey, Shali rice, and Vrihi rice.
These five are the primary sources/origins of wine (मद्याकर/wine sources); other origins produce secondary imitation wines (मद्य प्रतिरूपक/imitation liquor).
Understand the prominent qualities of wines based on their primary raw ingredient combinations (आकर संकर/ingredient combinations).
Shukta/Sour fermented gruel (शुक्त/fermented vinegar) aggravates bleeding, pitta, and kapha (रक्त पित्त कफ उत्क्लेदि/bleeding pitta kapha aggravator), but facilitates downward movement of vata (वातानुलोमन/vata movement facilitator).
Causes burning sensation (विदाही/causing burning), extremely sharp and hot (भृश तीक्ष्ण उष्ण/intensely sharp and hot), pleasing to heart (हृद्य/heart pleasing), appetizing (रुचिकर/taste booster), and laxative (सर/laxative).
Kindles digestion (दीपन/digestion booster), cold to touch (शिशिर स्पर्श/cooling to touch), cures anemia (पांडु/anemia), eye diseases (दृक्/vision disorders), and worms (कृमि/parasites).
Shukta prepared from jaggery (गुड/jaggery), sugarcane (इक्षु/sugarcane), wine (मद्य/wine), and grapes (मार्द्दीक/grape vinegar) are progressively lighter (लघु यथोत्तर/progressively lighter).
Similarly, sour fermented preparations from tubers (कंद), roots (मूल), and fruits (फल) should be understood.
Shandaki (शांडाकी/fermented vegetable preparation) and other sour fermented items (कालाम्ल/aged sour preparation) improve appetite (रोचन/appetite enhancer) and are light (लघु/light).
Dhanyamla/Fermented grain water (धान्याम्ल/fermented sour grain wash) is purgative (भेदि/purgative), sharp, hot, increases pitta (पित्तकृत्/pitta increaser), but cold to touch (स्पर्श शीतल/cooling to touch).
Relieves fatigue and exhaustion (श्रम क्लम हर/fatigue reliever), appetizing (रुच्य/appetizing), kindles digestion (दीपन/digestion enhancer), and cures bladder pain (बस्ति शूल जित्/bladder pain reliever).
Excellent in non-unctuous enema (आस्थापन/enema therapy), pleasing to heart (हृद्य/heart pleasing), light, and destroys vata and kapha (वात कफ पह/vata kapha pacifier).
Sauvira (सौवीरक/fermented huskless barley wash) and Tushodaka (तुषोदक/fermented husked barley wash) possess these same virtues.
They eradicate intestinal worms (कृमि/parasites), heart disease (हृद्रोग/heart disease), abdominal tumor (गुल्म/phantom tumor), hemorrhoids (अर्श/piles), and anemia (पांडुरोग/anemia).
These two are prepared sequentially from huskless barley (द्वितुष यव/unhusked barley) and husked barley (सतुष यव/husked barley).
Thus ends the classification of wines and fermented liquors (मद्यवर्ग/wine group).
Urine of cow (गो/cow), goat (अजा/goat), sheep (अवि/sheep), buffalo (महिषी/buffalo), elephant (गज/elephant), horse (अश्व/horse), camel (उष्ट्र/camel), donkey (खर/donkey).
Increases pitta (पित्तल/pitta increaser), dry (रूक्ष/dry), sharp (तीक्ष्ण/penetrating), hot (उष्ण/heating), salty-punctured (लवण/salty), laxative (सर/laxative), and pungent (कटु/pungent).
Overcomes worms (कृमि/parasites), edema (शोफ/swelling), abdominal disease (उदर/ascites), bloating (आनाह/distension), colic pain (शूल/pain), anemia (पांडु/anemia), kapha, and vata (कफ अनिल/kapha and vata).
Conquers abdominal tumor (गुल्म/phantom tumor), anorexia (अरुचि/loss of appetite), poison (विष/toxins), leucoderma (श्वित्र/vitiligo), skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin disorders), hemorrhoids (अर्श/piles), and is light (लघु/light).
Honored in purgation (विरेक/purgative therapy), non-unctuous enema (आस्थापन/enema therapy), ointments (आलेप/poultice application), and sweating therapy (स्वेद/sudation therapy).
Kindles digestion (दीपन/digestion booster), aids digestion (पाचन/digestive), purgative (भेदि/purgative); among all urines, cow urine (गोमूत्र/cow urine) is supreme (उत्तम/supreme).
Goat urine (छाग मूत्र/goat urine) cures dyspnea and cough (श्वास कास हर/asthma and cough reliever); instilling it in ears cures earache (कर्णशूल जित्/earache reliever).
Urine of elephant and horse (गज वाजि मूत्र/elephant and horse urine) should be administered in alkaline therapies (क्षार/alkaline medicine) and leucoderma (किलास/leucoderma).
Donkey urine (रासभ मूत्र/donkey urine) cures psychosis/insanity (उन्माद/madness), epilepsy (अपस्मार/fits), worms (कृमि/parasites), and diabetes/urinary disorders (मेह/diabetes).
Among animal dungs (शकृत्/animal dung), dung of these animals is astringent-bitter (कषाय तिक्त/astringent bitter), cures hiccups and dyspnea (हिक्का श्वास हर/hiccups and asthma reliever).
Deer dung (मार्ग शकृत्/deer dung) cures loss of vital essence (ओजःक्षय/immunity loss); bird dung (वैष्किर/bird dung) pacifies vata and kindles digestive fire (वात अग्नि नुत्/vata and fire booster).
Dung of carnivorous animals (प्रसह/carnivorous beasts) controls epilepsy (अपस्मार/fits) and psychosis (उन्माद/madness).
Gallbladder bile (पित्त/bile) of wild beasts (महामृग/large wild beasts) and aquatic animals (जलचारि/aquatic animals).
It cures skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin disorders), heart ailments (हृत्/heart disorders), eye diseases (नेत्ररोग/vision afflictions), and pacifies aggravated pitta (प्रवृद्ध पित्त/aggravated pitta).
Gallbladder bile is bitter (तिक्त/bitter), kills worms (कृमिहर/parasite destroyer), appetizing (रोचन/appetizing), and conquers kapha and vata (कफ वात जित्/kapha vata conqueror).
Human urine (मानुष मूत्र/human urine) is bitter (तिक्त/bitter), cures scabies/itch (पामाहर/scabies reliever), and destroys poison (विषापह/toxin destroyer).
Thus ends the classification of urines and animal products (मूत्रवर्ग/urine group).
In order, the classifications of water, milk, sugarcane, oil, wine, and urine have been described.
Thus, this section on liquid substances (द्रवैकदेश/liquid materials portion) has been explained in broad outline (यथास्थूल/broadly).
Thus ends the sixth chapter (षष्ठः अध्यायः/sixth chapter) named 'Knowledge of Liquid Substances' (द्रवद्रव्यविज्ञानीयः/knowledge of liquids) of Ashtanga Sangraha.




Now, therefore, we shall explain the chapter (अध्याय/section) on the nature of food substances (अन्नस्वरूप/properties of food materials).
Red rice (रक्त शालि/red rice), Mahashali (महान्/large rice), Sakalamaster, Turnaka, Shakunahrita rice varieties.
Saramukha, Dirghashuka (दीर्घशूक/long-awned rice), Rodhrashuka, Sugandhaka (सुगंधक/aromatic rice) varieties.
Pundra, Pandu, Pundarika, Pramoda, Gaura, Shariba rice types.
Kanchana, Mahisha, Shukadushaka, Kusumandaka grain varieties.
Langala, Lohavala, Kardama, Sheetabhiruka rice varieties.
Patanga, Tapaniya and other auspicious red shali rice grains (शालि/paddy varieties).
They have sweet taste and sweet post-digestive end-product (स्वादु रस/sweet taste), are unctuous (स्निग्ध/unctuous), aphrodisiac (वृष्य/virility booster), and form small hard stool (बद्ध अल्प वर्चस्/constipating).
They have astringent secondary taste (कषाय अनुरस/astringent post-taste), are wholesome (पथ्य/beneficial food), light to digest (लघु/light digestion), diuretic (मूत्रल/diuretic), and cold in potency (हिम/cooling).
Among awned grains (शूकधान्य/paddy grains), red rice (रक्त शालि/red paddy) is best, destroying thirst and all three humors (तृष्णा त्रिदोषहा/thirst and tridosha pacifier).
Next inferior to it is Mahashali, then Kalama rice, and then other varieties in decreasing order of quality.
Kalama rice, Patanga, Jhona, Swastika rice varieties are heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion).
Rice grown in dry land (धन्वा/dry region) is lighter, while rice grown in marshy land (आनूप/marshy land) is heavy.
Shashtika rice (षष्टिक/sixty-day rice) ripens in sixty days, is sweet (स्वादु/sweet), cold in potency (शीत/cooling), and pacifies all three humors (त्रिदोषघ्न/tridosha pacifier).
White Shashtika rice (गौर षष्टिक/white sixty-day rice) is superior, while black Shashtika rice (कृष्ण षष्टिक/black sixty-day rice) is slightly inferior.
Mahashashtika, Kordusha, Shyamaka, Nivara wild rice varieties.
Varaka, Uddalaka, Cheenaka, Kangu, Gavedhuka grain types.
They are astringent and sweet (कषाय मधुर/astringent-sweet), dry (रूक्ष/rough), pacify kapha and pitta (कफ पित्त हर/kapha-pitta pacifier), and increase vata humor (वातल/vata aggravating).
Barley (यव/barley) is dry (रूक्ष/rough), cold in potency (शीत/cooling), heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion), sweet (स्वादु/sweet), astringent (कषाय/astringent), and produces abundance of stool and flatus (बहु वर्चस्/stool generator).
Barley promotes stability (स्थैर्य/steadiness), kindles digestive fire (दीपन/appetite booster), enhances memory and intellect (मेधा/intelligence booster), and pacifies kapha and pitta (कफ पित्त जित्/kapha and pitta pacifier).
It cures urinary disorders (मूर्छ मेह/diabetes and urinary disease), rhinitis (पीनस/nasal catarrh), cough (कास/cough), asthma (श्वास/breathing difficulty), stiffness (स्तंभ/stiffness), and throat disorders (कंठ रोग/throat ailments).
Anuyava (अनुयव/small barley) is similar in properties to barley but slightly inferior in quality.
Wheat (गेहूँ/गोधूम/wheat) is sweet (स्वादु/sweet), heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion), cold in potency (शीत/cooling), nourishing (बृंहण/body builder), aphrodisiac (वृष्य/virility booster), and unctuous (स्निग्ध/unctuous).
Wheat unites fractured bones (संधानकृत्/fracture healer), provides strength (बल्य/strength giving), pacifies vata and pitta (वात पित्त ह्रा/vata pitta destroyer), and gives stability to body (स्थैर्यकृत्/steadiness provider).
Nandimukha wheat (नंदीमुख/horn-shaped wheat) is sweet (स्वादु/sweet), cold in potency (शीत/cooling), light to digest (लघु/light digestion), unctuous (स्निग्ध/unctuous), and wholesome (पथ्य/wholesome food).
Freshly harvested grains (नवीन अन्न/fresh grains) are heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion), while old stored grains (पुराण अन्न/aged grains) are light to digest (लघु/light digestion).
Grains that ripen quickly (शीघ्र पाक/fast ripening) are light, whereas late ripening grains (चिर पाक/slow ripening) are heavy.
Grains damaged by insects (कीट दष्ट/bug damaged), water logging, or frost are unwholesome (अहित/unwholesome).
Grains grown out of season or in improper soil lose their natural beneficial properties (गुण/qualities).
Freshly harvested rice (नवान्न/new rice) increases kapha humor (कफ/phlegm humor) and is heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion).
Grains stored for one year (एक संवत्सर उषित/one year aged) become light and wholesome (लघु पथ्य/light wholesome food).
Grains processed by roasting or frying (भृष्ट/roasted grains) become light to digest (लघु/light digestion) and pacify kapha (कफघ्न/kapha killer).
Puffed rice (लाजा/puffed paddy) is light to digest (लघु/light digestion), cold in potency (शीत/cooling), relieves thirst and vomiting (तृष्णा छर्दिघ्न/thirst vomiting reliever), and pacifies pitta and kapha (पित्त कफ शमन/pitta kapha pacifier).
Flattened rice (पृथुक/chiwra/beaten rice) is heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion), increases kapha (कफकर/kapha increaser), and causes constipation (विष्टंभी/constipating).
Saktu (सक्तु/roasted grain flour) is dry (रूक्ष/rough), light to digest (लघु/light digestion), satisfies hunger quickly (तर्पण/satiating), and kindles digestive fire (दीपन/appetite booster).
Saktu should not be eaten without water, nor eaten at night, nor taken after heavy meal, nor eaten twice in a day.
It should not be chewed dry, nor consumed with excess water, nor eaten without mixing ghee or sugar.
Germinated sprouted grains (अंकुरित/sprouted grains) are heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion), cause burning sensation (विदाही/burning sensation), and vitiate vision (दृष्टि दूषण/harmful for eyes).
Whole unhulled grains cause flatulence (आध्मान/abdominal gas) and increase vata humor (वात प्रकोपन/vata aggravating).
Husked clean grains (निस्तुष/hulled grains) are easy to digest (लघु/light digestion) and wholesome (पथ्य/beneficial).
Grains boiled with water until soft are light to digest (लघु/light digestion) and strengthen digestive fire (अग्निबल वर्धन/digestive power enhancer).
Grains cooked improperly or undercooked are heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion) and cause indigested toxic residue (आम/toxin formation).
Roasting on open fire (अंगार पक्व/charcoal baked) makes food light, while frying in oil makes it heavy.
Thus ends the classification of awned grains and paddies (शूकधान्यवर्ग/awned grain group).
Mudga (मूंग/green gram), Adhaki (अरहर/pigeon pea), Rajamasha (राजमा/kidney bean), Kulattha (कुलथी/horse gram), Nishpava (निष्पाव/flat bean), Masha (उड़द/black gram).
Tila (तिल/sesame), Chanaka (चना/chickpea), Masura (मसूर/red lentil), Kalaya (मटर/field pea), Harenu (हरेणु/small pea), and others belong to legumes (शिंबीधान्य/pulses group).
They are astringent and sweet in taste (कषाय मधुर/astringent-sweet), dry in nature (रूक्ष/rough), cold in potency (शीत/cooling), and constipating (ग्राहि/bowel binder).
They pacify kapha and pitta (कफ पित्त शमन/kapha and pitta reducer) and produce flatus and vata (वातल/vata aggravating).
Among pulses (शिंबीधान्य/legumes), Mudga (मूंग/green gram) is best; it is astringent-sweet (कषाय मधुर/astringent-sweet), dry (रूक्ष/rough), and cold in potency (शीत/cooling).
Green gram (मूंग/green gram) is light to digest (लघु/light digestion), beneficial for eyes (चक्षुष्य/vision booster), pacifies kapha and pitta (कफ पित्तघ्न/kapha-pitta destroyer), and does not aggravate vata excessively.
Green Mudga (हरी मूंग/green gram) is superior among all Mudga varieties.
Adhaki (अरहर/pigeon pea) pacifies kapha and pitta (कफ पित्तघ्न/kapha-pitta reducer), increases vata humor (वातल/vata increasing), and is light to digest (लघु/light digestion).
Rajamasha (राजमा/kidney bean) is heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion), increases vata humor (वातकर/vata increaser), unctuous (स्निग्ध/unctuous), and increases semen and milk (स्तन्य शुक्रकर/lactation and semen booster).
Kulattha (कुलथी/horse gram) is hot in potency (उष्ण/heating), sour in post-digestive taste (अम्ल पाक/sour end-product), removes urinary stones (अश्मरीघ्न/calculus breaker), and cures asthma and cough (श्वास कासघ्न/asthma cough reliever).
It pacifies vata and kapha (वात कफ शमन/vata-kapha pacifier), increases pitta and blood (पित्त रक्तप्रकोपण/pitta-blood aggravator), and cures hiccups (हिक्काघ्न/hiccup reliever).
Nishpava (निष्पाव/flat bean) increases pitta and blood (पित्त रक्त प्रकोप/pitta-blood increaser), causes burning sensation (विदाही/burning feeling), is hot in potency (उष्ण/heating), and obstructs channels (स्रोतोरोधी/channel blocker).
Masha (उड़द/black gram) is unctuous (स्निग्ध/unctuous), provides strength (बल्य/stamina booster), increases kapha and pitta (कफ पित्तकर/kapha-pitta increaser), and increases semen (शुक्रल/semen booster).
It is heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion), hot in potency (उष्ण/heating), sweet in taste (स्वादु/sweet), relieves vata humor (वातघ्न/vata destroyer), and promotes lactation (स्तन्यजनन/milk promoter).
Tila (तिल/sesame) is sweet, bitter, and astringent (स्वादु तिक्त कषाय/sweet bitter astringent), hot in potency (उष्ण/heating), unctuous (स्निग्ध/unctuous), and improves skin and hair (त्वच्य केश्य/skin hair nourisher).
It provides strength (बल्य/strength enhancer), increases intellect and digestive fire (मेधा अग्निप्रद/intellect digestive fire booster), and pacifies vata (वातघ्न/vata pacifier).
White sesame (श्वेत तिल/white sesame) is best, black sesame (कृष्ण तिल/black sesame) is medium, and multi-colored sesame is inferior.
Chanaka (चना/chickpea), Masura (मसूर/red lentil), Kalaya (मटर/pea), and Harenu are cold in potency (शीत/cooling), dry (रूक्ष/rough), and light to digest (लघु/light digestion).
They pacify kapha and pitta (कफ पित्तघ्न/kapha pitta reducer), increase vata humor (वातल/vata aggravating), and bind stools (स्रंसि/constipating).
Atasi (अलसी/flaxseed) is sweet and astringent (स्वादु कषाय/sweet astringent), hot in potency (उष्ण/heating), increases pitta and kapha (पित्त कफकर/pitta kapha increaser), and harms eyes and semen (दृष्टि शुक्रघ्न/harmful for vision and semen).
Kusumbha seeds (कुसुंभ/safflower seeds) are hot in potency (उष्ण/heating), cause burning sensation (विदाही/burning feeling), and vitiate all three humors (त्रिदोषकर/tridosha aggravating).
Freshly harvested legumes (नवान्न शिंबी/new pulses) are heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion), while old pulses (पुराण शिंबी/aged pulses) are light and wholesome (लघु पथ्य/light wholesome).
Legumes cooked without removing husk are heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion) and cause gas (आध्मानकर/flatulence causing).
Unhusked pulses split properly and boiled with water become easy to digest (लघु/light digestion).
Pulses roasted on fire (भृष्ट शिंबी/roasted pulses) become light (लघु/light) and pacify kapha and vata (कफ वातघ्न/kapha vata pacifier).
Soups made of green gram (मूंग यूष/green gram soup) are wholesome (पथ्य/beneficial), light to digest (लघु/light digestion), and good for eyes (चक्षुष्य/vision booster).
Soups made of horse gram (कुलत्थ यूष/horse gram soup) pacify vata and kapha (वात कफघ्न/vata kapha pacifier) and cure abdominal pain (शूलघ्न/pain reliever).
Soups prepared with ghee, spices, and pomegranate juice are called Kritayusha (कृतान्न/seasoned soup).
Soups prepared without oil, salt, or spices are called Akritayusha (अकृत/unseasoned soup).
Seasoned soup (कृत यूष/seasoned soup) kindles appetite (दीपन/digestion booster) and is light to digest (लघु/light digestion).
Unseasoned soup (अकृत यूष/plain soup) is extremely light (अति लघु/very light) and wholesome for sick persons (अतुर पथ्य/patient food).
Sprouted legume grains cause burning sensation (विदाही/burning feeling) and are heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion).
Thus ends the classification of pulses and legumes (शिंबीधान्यवर्ग/legumes group).
Manda (मंड/rice water broth), Peya (पेया/thin rice gruel), Vilepi (विलेपि/thick rice gruel), and Odana (ओदन/boiled rice) are prepared with water.
Manda (मंड/clear rice broth) is fourteen times water boiled with rice; it is lightest to digest (लघुतम/lightest) and kindles digestive fire (दीपनीय/appetite booster).
It relieves thirst and exhaustion (तृष्णा श्रमहर/thirst fatigue reliever), restores body channels (स्रोतो विशोधन/channel cleanser), and pacifies all humors (दोष ह्रा/humor balancer).
Peya (पेया/rice gruel) is thin gruel with small grain portion; it relieves hunger and thirst (क्षुधा तृष्णा हर/hunger thirst reliever) and promotes digestion (पाचनी/digestive booster).
Vilepi (विलेपि/thick rice gruel) is thick cooked gruel; it binds bowel (ग्राहि/bowel binder), satisfies hunger (तर्पणी/satiating), and nourishes heart (हृद्या/heart pleasing).
Odana (ओदन/boiled rice) cooked properly with drained water is light to digest (लघु/light digestion).
Rice cooked without draining water is heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion) and increases kapha (कफवर्धक/kapha increaser).
Rice fried in ghee before boiling (भृष्ट ओदन/ghee fried rice) is light to digest (लघु/light digestion) and appetizing (रोचन/flavorful).
Mamsa-rasa (मांस रस/meat soup) provides strength (बल्य/stamina booster), nourishes tissues (धातु वर्धन/tissue builder), and pacifies vata humor (वातघ्न/vata pacifier).
Kulmatha (कुल्माष/steamed half-cooked grains) is heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion), causes flatulence (आध्मानकर/gas causing), and increases vata (वात प्रकोपण/vata aggravator).
Saktu (सक्तु/roasted grain flour) mixed with ghee and water is refreshing (तर्पण/satiating) and strength giving (बलप्रद/strength provider).
Thus combinations and cooking processing (संयोग संस्कार/combination and processing) modify properties of food.
Vesavara (वेसवार/spiced minced meat mix) is heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion), unctuous (स्निग्ध/unctuous), provides strength (बल्य/strength giving), and increases kapha and pitta (कफ पित्त वर्धक/kapha pitta increaser).
Pupa (पूप/pancakes/cakes) cooked with jaggery and ghee are heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion) and nourishing (बृंहण/body builder).
Utkarika and Shashkuli (उत्कारिका शष्कुली/sweet puddings and fried pastries) are heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion) and increase kapha (कफकर/kapha increaser).
Kheer (क्षीर पायस/rice milk pudding) is heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion), nourishing (बृंहण/nourishing), and increases kapha and semen (कफ शुक्र वर्धक/kapha and semen booster).
Samyava and Ghritapura (सम्याव घृतपूर/ghee rich sweet pastries) provide strength (बलप्रद/strength giving) and are heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion).
Phenaka and Mathasurabhi (फेनक/fluffy fried pastries) are light, delicious (रोचन/flavorful), and pacify vata and pitta (वात पित्त शमन/vata pitta pacifier).
Dhānā (धाना/roasted grains) are heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion), dry (रूक्ष/rough), and stay long in stomach (विष्टंभी/constipating).
Laja (लाजा/puffed rice) mixed with sugar and honey relieves thirst, vomiting, and burning sensation (तृष्णा छर्दि दाहघ्न/thirst vomiting burning reliever).
Prithuka (पृथुक/flattened rice) soaked in milk is nourishing (बृंहण/nourishing) and increases strength (बलवर्धक/strength booster).
Raga and Shadava (राग षाडव/sour-sweet appetising condiments) kindle digestive fire (दीपन/digestion booster) and enhance taste (रुचिकर/taste enhancer).
They relieve anorexia (अरुचि/anorexia), vomiting (छर्दि/vomiting), and nausea (हृल्लास/nausea).
Ladduka (लड्डू/sweet balls) made of gram flour and ghee are heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion), nourishing (बृंहण/body builder), and aphrodisiac (वृष्य/virility booster).
Modaka (मोदक/sweet dumplings) increase kapha (कफकर/kapha increaser), provide satisfaction (तुष्टिप्रद/satisfaction giving), and increase strength (बल्य/strength enhancer).
Vatika (वटक/fried lentil dumplings) are heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion), cause gas (आध्मानकर/gas causing), and increase vata and pitta (वात पित्तकर/vata pitta increaser).
Soup prepared with buttermilk (तक्र सिद्ध यूष/buttermilk soup) is light to digest (लघु/light digestion) and cures piles and indigestion (अर्शो मंदीघ्न/piles digestion reliever).
Kambalika and Khada (कंबलिक खड/sour spiced buttermilk preparations) kindle digestive fire (दीपन/appetite booster) and pacify vata and kapha (वात कफ शमन/vata kapha pacifier).
Kanjika (कांजिक/fermented sour gruel) is light to digest (लघु/light digestion), burning sensation reliever (दाहघ्न/burning reliever), and digestive (पाचन/digestive aid).
Panaka (पानक/flavored sweet drinks) prepared with fruit juices and sugar relieve thirst and fatigue (तृष्णा श्रमहर/thirst fatigue reliever).
Drinks prepared with lemon and sugar (अम्ल पानक/sour sweet drink) are heart-pleasing (हृद्य/heart pleasing) and appetizing (रोचन/taste booster).
Panaka prepared with tamarind or mango is heavy to digest (गुरु/heavy digestion) and increases kapha (कफकर/kapha increaser).
Cold water mixed with jaggery and pepper (गुड़ोदक/jaggery water) kindles appetite (दीपन/digestion booster) and relieves dysuria (मूत्रकृच्छ्रघ्न/dysuria reliever).
Syrups prepared with pomegranate juice (दाडिम पानक/pomegranate drink) pacify pitta (पित्तशामक/pitta pacifier) and cure diarrhea (अतिसारघ्न/diarrhea reliever).
Drinks prepared with vetiver and sandalwood (उशीर चंदन पानक/cooling herbal drink) relieve fever, burning, and thirst (ज्वर दाह तृष्णाघ्न/fever burning thirst reliever).
