Disease-opposite therapies (व्याधिविपरीत/disease specific) include oleation, sudation, five purifications (पंचकर्म/five purificatory therapies), medicated smoking, eye salves, and surgical incisions.
Surgical softening, warm poultices, and incisions are also applied in specific localized swellings.
Disease-specific remedies include Musta-Parpataka for fever (ज्वर/fever), and turmeric (रजनी/turmeric) with barley food for diabetes (प्रमेह/obstinate urinary disorders/diabetes).
In upward bleeding disorders (ऊर्ध्व रक्तपित्त/upward bleeding disease), purgation is given, whereas in downward bleeding, emesis is administered.
Dual-action remedies (उभयार्थकारि/dual purpose) act simultaneously against both the cause and the disease symptoms.
Examples include emetics for vomiting, laxatives for diarrhea, wine for alcoholism, heat for burns, and spicy warm foods to bring hidden pitta to its path.
Applying cold water externally when kapha is hidden inside pushes internal heat inward to melt rigid phlegm.
Such non-contrary proper medical treatments (सद्भेषज/true medicine) successfully achieve cause-opposite and disease-opposite results.
Unwholesome non-medicines (अनौषध/harmful substances) are divided into two types: immediate afflictors and delayed afflictors.
Immediate afflictors cause instant harm or death (सद्यःप्राणहर/fatal shock), while delayed afflictors cause gradual systemic damage over time.
Immediate afflictors destroy vitality instantly (बाधन/acute damage).
Delayed afflictors produce chronic deterioration in organs after a period of time (अनुबाधन/chronic distress).
Now, the specific properties of medicines based on taste (रस/nutrient plasma/taste), potency (वीर्य/potency), and metabolic post-digestive effect (विपाक/post-digestive effect) are described.
Gold (सुवर्ण/gold) is nourishing (बृंहण/stamina builder), unctuous, sweet in taste and post-digestive effect, cold, and a rejuvenator (रसायन/rejuvenative elixir).
Gold destroys poisons (विषदोषघ्न/toxin destroyer), is cooling in potency (शीत/cold), astringent, and acts as a supreme rejuvenator.
Silver (रूप्य/silver) is unctuous, astringent-sour, sweet after digestion, restores youthfulness (वयःस्थापन/anti-aging rejuvenator), cooling, scraping, and pacifies vata and pitta.
Silver preserves youthful vitality (वयःस्थापन/age sustainer), is cold, scraping (लेखन/fat reducer), and overcomes vata and pitta disorders.
Copper (ताम्र/copper) is bitter-sweet, astringent, scraping (लेखन/fat reducer), light, pungent after digestion, cold, healing, and pacifies kapha and pitta.
Copper promotes wound healing (रोपण/wound healer), is cold in potency, pungent after digestion, and pacifies kapha and pitta.
Bronze (कांस्य/bronze) has an astringent secondary taste, is clear, scraping, light, improves vision (दृष्टिप्रसादन/vision clarifier), dry, bitter, and overcomes pitta and kapha.
Bronze clarifies eyesight (दृष्टिप्रसादन/vision cleanser), is dry (रूक्ष/dry), bitter, and pacifies pitta and kapha.
Lead and zinc alloys are slightly bitter and salty, purgative (भेदि/purgative), cure anemia (पांडु/anemia/paleness), parasites, and vata.
Lead is scraping (लेखन/scraping), increases pitta slightly, and shares similar medicinal attributes with bronze and zinc.
Black iron (कृष्णलोह/magnetic iron) is beneficial for eyes (चक्षुष्य/vision enhancer), astringent, sweet-bitter, scraping, vata-increasing, and cold.
Black iron cures skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin diseases/leprosy), parasites, and kapha, scraping away fat tissue and internal obstructions.
Black iron cures body flaccidity, premature graying of hair (पालित्य/premature graying of hair), anemia (पांडु/anemia/paleness), consumption (शोष/tissue wasting), and swelling (शोफ/swelling/edema).
Sharp steel (तीक्ष्ण लोह/processed steel) acts with intense potency, is deep-penetrating (विकाषि/tissue loosening), and heavy to digest.
Precious gems like ruby (पद्मराग/ruby), sapphire (महानील/sapphire), topaz (पुष्पराग/topaz), emerald, pearl, coral, diamond, and quartz,
Gems including pearl (मुक्ता/pearl), coral (विद्रुम/coral), diamond (वज्र/diamond), lapis lazuli (वैडूर्य/lapis lazuli), and quartz (स्फटिक/crystal),
Gemstones (मणिरत्न/precious gems) are fluid, cold, astringent, sweet, scraping, and clarify vision.
Wearing precious gems (मणिधारण/gem wearing) protects against evil influences, poverty, bad luck, and toxic poisons.
Wearing gems brings auspiciousness, longevity (आयुष्य/long life), vital essence (ओजः/vital essence), joy, enthusiasm, and spiritual peace.
Glass (काच/glass) contains alkali (क्षार/alkali), is heating in potency, and improves eyesight when used as eye salves.
Conch shell (शंख/conch shell) and mother of pearl (समुद्रमल्ल/oyster shell) are cold, astringent, and highly scraping (लेखन/scraping).
Blue vitriol (तुत्थक/copper sulfate) is pungent, alkaline, astringent, clear, light, scraping, purgative, eye cleanser, and destroys itching, worms, and poison.
Blue vitriol cures skin itching (कंडू/itching), intestinal worms (कृमि/parasites), and neutralizing toxic poisons.
Red ochre (गैरिक/red ochre) is clear, unctuous, astringent-sweet, cold, and pacifies kapha.
Realgar (मनःशिला/realgar) is bitter-pungent, scraping (लेखन/scraping), fluid, and pacifies kapha.
Orpiment (हरिताल/orpiment) is unctuous, astringent-pungent, and destroys toxic poisons (विष/poison/toxin).
Red lead (सिंदूर/vermilion) is heating, light, cures skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin diseases/leprosy), wound ulcers, and toxic poison.
Cinnabar (हिंगुल/cinnabar) is heating, light, cures skin diseases, and acts like quicksilver (पारद/purified mercury).
Purified mica (अभ्रक/mica) is sweet after digestion, cooling, nourishing, cures anemia, and acts as a supreme rejuvenator (रसायन/rejuvenative elixir).
Purified quicksilver (पारद/mercury) overcomes all six tastes, pacifies all three humors (त्रिदोष/three bodily humors), cures all diseases, and provides immortality when processed.
Black stibium antimony (अंजन/antimony) is cooling, astringent, stops bleeding, cures eye diseases (नेत्र रोग/eye diseases), and heals skin ulcers.
Saurashtri clay (सौराष्ट्री/alum earth) is astringent, sour, scraping, cures throat diseases, and stops external hemorrhages.
Purified sulfur (गंधक/sulfur) is pungent, heating, unctuous, kindles digestion, cures skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin diseases/leprosy), parasites, and itching.
Bituminous asphaltum Shilajit (शिलाजतु/shilajit) is bitter, astringent, pungent after digestion, not overly hot, and cures all chronic diseases.
Shilajit cures diabetes (मेहा/diabetes), skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin diseases/leprosy), hemorrhoids (अर्शः/hemorrhoids/piles), swelling (शोफ/swelling/edema), anemia, and urinary calculi.
Purified Aconite Vatsanabha (वत्सनाभ/aconite poison) is sharp, hot, pervading, Yogavahi, and cures severe tridosha fevers when combined in minute doses.
Aconite poison (वत्सनाभ/aconite poison) acts as a life-saving medicine when processed properly with milk and ghee.
Alkalies like Yavakshara (यवक्षार/barley ash) and Swarjikakshara are sharp, hot, pungent, digest unripened chyme, and destroy urinary stones (अश्मरी/urinary calculi).
Alkali preparations dissolve abdominal tumors (गुल्म/phantom tumor), ascites (उदर/ascites/abdominal enlargement), spleen enlargement, and internal urinary gravel.
Rock salt (सैंधव/rock salt) is sweet, light, unctuous, beneficial for eyes (चक्षुष्य/vision booster), kindles digestion, and pacifies all three humors.
Black salt (सौवर्चल/black salt) is light, fragrant, sweet after digestion, digestive, clears belching, and cures abdominal pain.
Sea salt (समुद्र लवण/sea salt) is sweet after digestion, heavy, increases kapha, mild laxative, and pacifies vata.
Siddhalavana and Vida salt are sharp, hot, penetrating, digest food, cure flatulence (आध्मान/flatulence bloating), and relieve colic pain.
Audbhida salt (औद्भिद/earthen salt) is slightly sharp, alkaline, bitter, light, penetrating, and relieves urinary retention.
Romaka salt (रोमक/salt from Sambhar lake) is light, sharp, laxative, penetrating, and quickly clears body channels.
All salt varieties (लवण/salty taste) are generally unctuous, digestive, laxative, sharp, and aid food movement down the gut.
Salt in excess destroys eyesight, causes premature graying of hair, baldness, bleeding disorders, and skin eruptions.
Chebulic myrobalan Haritaki (हरीतकी/haritaki) possesses five tastes excluding salt, is sweet after digestion, heating, light, and rejuvenative.
Haritaki cures constipation, flatulence, anemia, heart disease, skin disorders, asthma, cough, and swelling.
Bibhitaki (विभीतक/baheda) is astringent, sweet after digestion, cold, light, cures cough, eye diseases, and kapha-pitta.
Amalaki (आमलकी/amla) possesses five tastes excluding salt, is cold, light, sweet after digestion, pacifies all tridoshas, and enhances vision.
Amalaki is a supreme rejuvenator (रसायन/rejuvenative elixir), cures bleeding pitta, diabetes (मेह/urinary disorder/diabetes), burning sensation (दाह/burning sensation), and fever.
The combination of Haritaki, Bibhitaki, and Amalaki is called Triphala (त्रिफला/three fruits).
Triphala cures eye diseases (नेत्र रोग/eye diseases), skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin diseases/leprosy), diabetes, sluggish digestion, obesity, and fever.
Dry ginger Shunthi (शुंठी/dry ginger) is sweet after digestion, unctuous, heating, kindles fire, aphrodisiac, and pacifies vata and kapha.
Black pepper Maricha (मरिच/black pepper) is pungent, light, dry, heating, destroys kapha and vata, and cures anorexia.
Long pepper Pippali (पिप्पली/long pepper) is sweet after digestion, unctuous, not overly hot, rejuvenating, and cures cough and asthma.
Fresh Pippali is heavy, increasing kapha and dampness, whereas dry Pippali is light, digestive, and pacifies vata-kapha.
The combination of Shunthi, Maricha, and Pippali is known as Trikatu (त्रिकटु/three acrids) or Vyosha.
Trikatu kindles digestive fire, cures obesity (मेदः/fat tissue), rhinitis (पीनस/chronic rhinitis), cough, asthma, skin diseases, and sluggish metabolism.
Cinnamon bark Twak (त्वक्/cinnamon) is sweet-pungent, fragrant, light, heating, pacifies vata-kapha, and cleanses the mouth.
Cardamom Ela (एला/cardamom) is pungent, light, cold, cures cough, asthma, dysuria, hemorrhoids, and clears mouth odor.
Tejpatra (पत्र/bay leaf) is light, fragrant, warm, kindles digestion, and cures rhinitis and loss of taste.
Nagakeshara (नागकेशर/ironwood flower) is astringent, light, fragrant, cold, cures bleeding piles, swelling, and pitta-kapha.
The combination of Twak, Ela, and Tejpatra is known as Trijataka (त्रिजातक/three aromatics), and with Nagakeshara as Chaturjata.
Chaturjata enhances taste, kindles digestive fire, improves complexion (वर्ण्य/complexion enhancer), and clears mouth malodor.
Cumin Jeeraka (जीरक/cumin seed) is pungent, light, dry, heating, digestive, improves appetite, and pacifies vata-kapha.
Black cumin Krishna-jeeraka (कृष्णजीरा/caraway) is sharp, hot, fragrant, digestive, and cures abdominal colic (शूल/colic pain).
Coriander Dhanyaka (धान्यक/coriander) is sweet-astringent, light, unctuous, cooling, digestive, and relieves thirst and burning sensation.
Fennel Shatapushpa (शतपुष्पा/fennel) is sweet-pungent, heating, light, promotes eyesight, kindles fire, and pacifies vata-kapha.
Ajwain Yavani (यवानी/carom seed) is sharp, hot, pungent, digestive, cures abdominal gas, colic pain, and intestinal worms.
Mustard Sarshapa (सर्षप/mustard seed) is sharp, hot, pungent, unctuous, increases pitta, and cures parasites and skin disorders.
Asafoetida Hingu (हिंगु/asafoetida) is sharp, hot, pungent, digestive, relieves abdominal distension (आध्मान/flatulence bloating), and pacifies vata-kapha.
Guduchi (गुडूची/giloy) is bitter, astringent-sweet, sweet after digestion, heating, rejuvenative (रसायन/rejuvenative elixir), and pacifies all three humors.
Guduchi cures fevers (ज्वर/fever), gout (वातरक्त/gouty arthritis), jaundice (कामला/jaundice), diabetes, burning sensation, and vomiting.
Neem Nimba (निंब/neem) is bitter, cold, light, cures skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin diseases/leprosy), parasites, diabetes, fever, and excess pitta-kapha.
Katuki (कटुकी/picrorhiza) is bitter, cold, light, purgative, cures liver disorders, jaundice, fever, and bleeding pitta.
Chitraka (चित्रक/leadwort) is pungent, sweet after digestion, hot, sharp, kindles digestive fire supreme, and cures hemorrhoids (अर्शः/hemorrhoids/piles).
Bhallataka (भल्लातक/marking nut) is hot, sharp, unctuous, sweet after digestion, destroys vata-kapha, tumors, and skin diseases.
Aragvadha (आरग्विध/cassia fistula) is sweet, cold, mild purgative (स्रंसन/mild laxative), cures skin diseases, fever, heart disease, and abdominal pain.
Punarnava (पुनर्नवा/hogweed) is astringent-bitter-sweet, light, heating, diuretic, cures swelling (शोफ/swelling/edema), anemia, and heart disorders.
Gokshura (गोक्षुर/puncture vine) is sweet, cooling, unctuous, aphrodisiac (वृष्य/aphrodisiac/virility enhancer), cures dysuria (मूत्रकृच्छ्र/painful urination/dysuria), and urinary stones (अश्मरी/urinary calculi).
Ashwagandha (अश्वगंधा/indian ginseng) is bitter-sweet, hot, unctuous, aphrodisiac, nourishing, rejuvenative, and pacifies vata-kapha.
Shatavari (शतावरी/wild asparagus) is sweet-bitter, cold, unctuous, nourishing, increases breast milk (स्तन्य/breast milk), sperms, and pacifies vata-pitta.
Bala (बला/country mallow) is sweet, unctuous, cold, strengthening (बल्य/strength promoting), cures bleeding disorders, and pacifies vata-pitta.
Atibala (अतिबला/abutilon) shares properties with Bala, being cold, unctuous, strengthening, and curative of emaciation.
Nagabala (नागबला/nagbala) is sweet, astringent, strengthening, nourishing, and cures chest wound consumption (क्षतक्षीण/debilitated by chest injury).
Vidari (विदारी/kudzu tuber) is sweet, cold, heavy, unctuous, aphrodisiac, increases milk production, and promotes body bulk.
Varahikanda (वाराहीकंद/wild yam) is bitter-sweet, heating, rejuvenating, increases strength, and pacifies vata-kapha.
Kapikachhu (कपिकच्छू/velvet bean) is sweet, unctuous, heavy, cold, supreme aphrodisiac (वृष्य/aphrodisiac/virility enhancer), and pacifies vata disorders.
Yashtimadhu Licorice (यष्टिमधु/licorice) is sweet, cold, unctuous, good for eyes, cleanses wounds, and improves voice and complexion.
Vasa (वासा/malabar nut) is bitter-astringent, cold, light, cures cough, asthma, bleeding pitta (रक्तपित्त/bleeding disease), and fever.
Kantakari (कंटकारी/yellow-berried nightshade) is pungent-bitter, hot, light, cures cough, asthma, rhinitis, and chest colic.
Brihati (बृहती/indian nightshade) is bitter-pungent, hot, digestive, cures anorexia, cough, intestinal worms, and vata-kapha.
Shalaparni (शालपर्णी/desmodium) is sweet-bitter, hot, unctuous, strengthens the heart, cures fever, vomiting, and asthma.
Prishniparni (पृश्निपर्णी/uraria) is sweet-digestive, hot, unctuous, stops bleeding, heals fractures, and pacifies vata-pitta.
The group of Desmodium, Uraria, Brihati, Kantakari, and Gokshura forms Laghu-Panchamula (लघुपांचमूल/five lesser roots).
Bilva (बिल्व/bael tree root) is bitter-astringent, hot, unctuous, kindles digestion, cures dysentery, and pacifies vata-kapha.
Agnimantha (अग्निमंथ/premma) is bitter-pungent, hot, light, cures swelling (शोफ/swelling/edema), anemia, and pacifies kapha-vata.
Shyonaka (श्योनाक/oroxylum) is astringent-bitter, hot, light, cures diarrhea, bleeding, and kindles digestive fire.
Gambhari (गंभारी/gmelina) is sweet-bitter, cold, heavy, aphrodisiac, cures fever, burning sensation, and thirst.
Patala (पाटला/trumpet flower) is astringent-sweet, hot, light, cures swelling, hiccup, vomiting, and bleeding pitta.
The roots of Bilva, Agnimantha, Shyonaka, Gambhari, and Patala form Brihat-Panchamula (बृहत्पांचमूल/five greater roots).
Combining Laghu-Panchamula and Brihat-Panchamula creates Dashamula (दशमूल/ten roots), which cures all vata disorders, asthma, and cough.
Musta (मुस्त/nutgrass) is bitter-astringent, cold, light, digestive, stops diarrhea, and pacifies kapha-pitta fevers.
Chandana (चंदन/sandalwood) is bitter-sweet, cold, light, fragrant, cures burning sensation, fever, bleeding pitta, and toxic states.
Usheera (उशीर/vetiver) is bitter-sweet, cold, light, stops sweating, cures burning sensation, thirst, and pitta fevers.
Padmaka (पद्मक/wild cherry wood) is astringent-sweet, cold, cures bleeding pitta, skin eruptions, and burning sensation.
Manjistha (मंजिष्ठा/indian madder) is sweet-bitter, astringent, heating, cleanses blood (रक्तशोधन/blood purifier), and cures skin diseases.
Sarit-Sativa Sariva (सारिवा/indian sarsaparilla) is sweet-bitter, cold, heavy, purifies blood and breast milk, and cures fever.
Anantamula (अनंतमूल/black sarsaparilla) cures burning sensation, loss of appetite, dyspnea, cough, and toxic poison.
Lodhra (लोध्र/symplocos bark) is astringent, cold, light, stops hemorrhages, cures diarrhea, and heals eye disorders.
Dhataki flowers (धातकी/fire flame bush) are astringent, cold, light, cure bleeding disorders, diarrhea, and skin ulcers.
Kutaja (कुटज/conessi bark) is bitter-astringent, cold, dry, light, cures severe dysentery (अतिसार/diarrhea), bleeding piles, and fever.
Indrayava (इंद्रयव/kutaja seeds) cures dysentery, intestinal worms, fever, skin diseases, and abdominal pain.
Karanja (करंज/pongame oil tree) is bitter-pungent, hot, sharp, cures skin diseases, parasites, hemorrhoids, and ulcers.
Arka (अर्क/madar plant) is sharp, hot, pungent, purgative, cures abdominal tumors (गुल्म/phantom tumor/mass), skin lesions, and poisons.
Snuhi (स्नुही/euphorbia latex) is sharp, hot, strong purgative (रेचनम्/purgative evacuation), cures ascites (उदर/ascites/abdominal enlargement), swelling, and deep toxicities.
Haridra Turmeric (हरिद्रा/turmeric) is bitter-pungent, hot, dry, purifies blood, improves skin tone, and cures diabetes and skin disorders.
Daruharidra (दारुहरिद्रा/tree turmeric) shares properties with turmeric, curing eye diseases (नेत्र रोग/eye diseases), jaundice, and skin ulcers.
Vacha (वचा/sweet flag) is sharp, hot, pungent, emetic, restores speech, memory, intellect, and cures abdominal gas.
Jatamansi (जटामांसी/spikenard) is bitter-sweet, cold, fragrant, calms the mind (मेध्य/brain tonic), relieves insanity (उन्माद/insanity/mania), and pitta-vata.
Tagara (तगर/indian valerian) is sweet-bitter, hot, unctuous, calms neurological pain, epilepsy, and toxic poison.
Devadaru (देवदारु/himalayan cedar) is bitter-pungent, hot, unctuous, cures cough, asthma, rhinitis, swelling, and vata disorders.
Rasna (रास्ना/pluchea) is bitter-astringent, hot, heavy, supreme pacifier of vata disorders (वातहर/vata pacifier), and joint pain.
Eranda Castor root (एरण्ड/castor root) is sweet-pungent, hot, unctuous, clears pelvic pain, backache, and abdominal gas.
Pippalimula (पिप्पलीमूल/long pepper root) is pungent, hot, sharp, digestive, kindles appetite, and cures abdominal bloating.
Chavya (चव्य/cubeb root) is pungent, hot, sharp, digestive, cures kapha-vata disorders, abdominal gas, and piles.
Gajapippali (गजपिप्पली/large long pepper) is pungent, hot, digestive, cures throat diseases, asthma, and kapha ailments.
Shankhapushpi (शंखपुष्पी/bindweed) is bitter, fluid, supreme brain tonic (मेध्य/brain tonic), restores memory, and cures insomnia.
Brahmi (ब्राह्मी/water hyssop) is bitter, cold, light, supreme memory booster (मेध्य/brain tonic), cures speech defects, and mental disorders.
Mandukaparni (मंडूकपर्णी/gotu kola) is sweet-bitter, cold, light, rejuvenating, enhances intellect, and improves complexion.
Jyotishmati (ज्योतिष्मती/celastrus) is sharp, hot, pungent, kindles intellect (मेध्य/brain tonic), improves memory, and cures kapha-vata.
Kurchi and Kutaja seeds act as potent digestive astringents in chronic gut conditions.
Sandalwood, Usheera, and Valaka decoctions cure fever, vomiting, diarrhea, thirst, and internal hemorrhages.
Licorice Yashtimadhu cleanses wound ulcers, heals internal tissues, and promotes vocal clarity and skin luster.
Turmeric and tree turmeric purify skin pores, cure itching, eczema, skin ulcers, and toxic insect bites.
Prapaundarika is cooling, heals eye disorders, heals wound ulcers, and calms bleeding pitta.
The three Bala varieties (Bala, Atibala, Nagabala) strengthen weak tissues, cure chest injury, and restore vitality.
Betel leaf Tambula (तांबूल/betel leaf) is pungent, alkaline, pleasant, hot, cleanses throat, removes oral malodor, and pacifies kapha.
Areca nut Puga (पूग/betel nut) is astringent, sweet, scraping, clears excess salivation, but in excess causes confusion.
Jatiphala (जातीफल/nutmeg) and Jatipatri (जातीपत्री/mace) are light, fragrant, heart-pleasing, cure thirst, and remove mouth malodor.
Kankola (कंकोल/cubeb berries) and Lavanga (लवंग/cloves) are light, fragrant, eye-pleasing, relieve thirst, and stimulate appetite.
Camphor Karpura (कर्पूर/camphor) is sweet-bitter, cold, fragrant, light, clears phlegm, and relieves burning sensation.
Lata-kasturi (लताकस्तूरी/musk mallow) is light, fragrant, cold, cures mouth dryness (मुखशोष/dryness of mouth), and enhances taste.
Lotus flower Padma (पद्म/lotus) is astringent-sweet, cold, pacifies pitta, blood disorders (रक्तपित्त/bleeding disease), and burning sensation.
Bakula, Punnaga, Kumuda, Utpala, and Patala flowers share cooling, astringent, and pitta-pacifying qualities.
Champaka and Kimshuka flowers are cooling, astringent, and cure bleeding disorders and skin eruptions.
Jati and Mallika jasmine flowers are bitter, cooling, light, and pacify vata and toxic pitta.
Nagakeshara and Sindhuvara flowers cure toxic states, skin disorders, and pitta imbalances.
Ketaki flower is bitter, cooling, light, clears head phlegm, and cures skin itching.
Shirisha flower is supreme antidote for all toxic poisons (विषहर/poison neutralizer), swelling, and insect stings.
Agastya flower (अगस्त्य/hummingbird tree flower) is astringent, cold, cures night blindness (नक्तांध्य/night blindness) when used as nasal drops.
Agastya flower nasal drops (नस्य/nasal administration) cure fourth-day intermittent malaria fever (चतुर्थक ज्वर/quartan fever).
Bandhuka and Yuthika flowers are astringent, cooling, and pacify kapha and pitta.
Saffron Kunkuma (कुंकुम/saffron) is hot in potency, improves skin complexion (वर्ण्य/complexion enhancer), cleanses wounds, and pacifies tridosha.
Bakuchi seeds (बाकुची/babchi) cure leucoderma (श्वित्रम्/leucoderma/vitiligo), skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin diseases/leprosy), parasites, and kapha-vata disorders.
Edible seed oils and herbal powders promote facial glow, softness, and skin youthfulness.
Walking on foot (चक्रमण/walking exercise) within limits increases longevity, kindles digestive fire, and clears body channels.
Walking promotes easy downward movement of vata (अनुलोमन/downward movement), relieves stiffness, fatigue, and foot weakness.
Footwear (पादत्र/protective footwear) protects feet, enhances vision, preserves semen, promotes strength, and keeps off reptiles and thorns.
Carrying an umbrella (छत्र/umbrella protection) protects against sun, rain, wind, dust, enhances skin complexion, and preserves health.
Direct harsh wind (प्रवात/exposure to strong wind) causes dryness, loss of skin tone, stiffness, burning sensation, thirst, and fatigue.
Sheltered breeze (अप्रवात/sheltered from wind) overcomes fatigue, kindles digestive fire, cures fainting, and brings physical comfort.
East wind (प्राग्वायु/easterly wind) is hot, damp, causes skin diseases, piles, toxic states, worms, and aggravates tridosha fevers.
West wind (पश्चिम वायु/westerly wind) is cool, clear, relieves fainting, burning sensation, thirst, and toxic states.
South wind (दक्षिण वायु/southerly wind) is supreme, beneficial for eyes, enhances body strength, calms bleeding pitta, and does not irritate vata.
North wind (उत्तर वायु/northerly wind) is unctuous, gentle, sweet-astringent, cold, and keeps all three humors peaceful.
Excessive sun exposure (आतप/sunlight exposure) causes dizziness, thirst, sweating, burning sensation, fainting, loss of skin color, and bleeding disorders.
Cool shade (छाया/shade protection) removes all ill effects of heat, sun exposure, sweating, thirst, and burning sensation.
Darkness (तमस्/dark ignorance mental quality) has astringent-pungent effect, whereas moonlight (ज्योत्स्ना/moonlight exposure) is sweet, cooling, and refreshing.
Auspicious herbs and gem therapies protect life, preserve mental clarity, and ward off hidden diseases.
Properties of medicines are thus outlined briefly based on tastes (रस/nutrient plasma/taste), potencies (वीर्य/potency), and post-digestive effects (विपाक/post-digestive effect).
There is no substance in the universe that cannot be used as medicine when applied with proper knowledge and reasoning.
Proper identification of plants (रूपविज्ञान/symptom knowledge) should be learned directly from forest dwellers, cowherds, and experienced traditional collectors.
A wise physician should study herbal forms, habitats, seasons of harvesting, and processing methods with diligence.
Correct knowledge of drugs combined with scientific application leads to complete cure of diseases.
A physician who knows drugs by name, form, and properties, and uses them according to situation, is called a true healer (भिषक्/physician).
Knowledge of various medicines is thus declared in brief to guide the intelligent physician in formulating cures.
By understanding drug principles, one can compound suitable remedies for every disease state.
Thus ends the twelfth chapter (द्वादशः अध्यायः/twelfth chapter) named 'Classification of Medicinal Herbs and Therapies' (विविधौषधविज्ञानीयः/knowledge of medicines) of Ashtanga Sangraha.




Now, therefore, we shall explain the chapter (अध्याय/section) on the compilation of primary best remedies (अग्र्य संग्रह/primary best substances).
Pure water (उदक/water) is the best (श्रेष्ठ/foremost) for refreshing (आश्वासन/reassuring), arresting discharges (स्तंभन/styping), and moistening (क्लेदन/moistening).
Bathing (स्नान/bathing) and wine (सुरा/wine) are the best for relieving fatigue (श्रमहर/fatigue buster).
Milk (क्षीर/milk) is the best among vitalizing substances (जीवनीय/vitality restoring).
Meat (मांस/meat) is the best among nourishing body-building substances (बृंहणीय/body nourisher).
Meat soup (रस/meat broth) is the best among pleasing refreshing foods (प्रीणन/satiating).
Salt (लवण/salt) is the best among taste enhancers for non-goat food items (रुचिकर/flavor enhancer).
Tinduka fruit (तिंदुक/tinduka fruit) is the best among taste improvers for goat meat items (रुचिकर/taste booster).
Sour taste (अम्ल/sour flavor) is the best among heart-pleasing substances (हृद्य/cardiac pleasing).
Chicken meat (कुक्कुट/rooster meat) is the best among strength-promoting foods (बल्य/stamina booster).
Oil (तैल/sesame oil) is the best for pacifying vata and kapha humors (वात कफ हर/vata-kapha pacifier).
Ghee (सर्पिः/clarified butter) is the best for pacifying vata and pitta humors (वात पित्त हर/vata-pitta pacifier).
Honey (मधु/honey) is the best for pacifying kapha and pitta humors (कफ पित्त हर/kapha-pitta pacifier).
Sudation therapy (स्वेद/sweating treatment) is the best for bringing softness (मार्दव/softness).
Physical exercise (व्यायाम/exercise) is the best for creating body stability (स्थैर्य/steadiness).
Alkalies (क्षार/alkaline ashes) are the most damaging to virility and potency (पुंस्त्व घाती/virility reducer).
Unripe Kapittha fruit (आम कपित्थ/unripe wood apple) is the worst for throat health (अकंठ्य/harmful to throat).
Sheep ghee (आविक सर्पिः/sheep butter) is the worst among heart-pleasing foods (अहृद्य/unpleasing to heart).
Buffalo milk (महिषी क्षीर/buffalo milk) is the best for inducing deep sleep (स्वप्नजनन/sleep inducer).
Incompletely formed curd (मंदक दधि/unformed curd) is the foremost in causing channel obstruction (अभिष्यंद/channel clogging).
Sugarcane (इक्षु/sugarcane) is the best for increasing urinary output (मूत्रजनन/diuretic).
Barley (यव/barley) is the best for increasing fecal bulk (पुरीषजनन/fecal bulk increaser).
Jamun fruit (जांबव/java plum) is the foremost in aggravating vata humor (वातजनन/vata increaser).
Horse gram (कुलत्थ/kulthi dal) is the foremost in producing hyperacidity (अम्लपित्त/hyperacidity generator).
Black gram (माष/urad dal), fried pastries (शष्कुली/fried cake), and sheep milk increase pitta and kapha (पित्त कफ/pitta-kapha increasers).
Duralabha herb (दुरालभा/duralabha) is the best for drying up pitta and kapha (पित्त कफ शोषक/pitta-kapha drier).
Fasting (उपवास/fasting) is the best therapy for relieving fever (ज्वरहर/fever reducer).
Vasaka herb (वृष/vasaka) is the best for pacifying bleeding disorders (रक्तपित्त हर/bleeding pacifier).
Kantakari herb (कंटकारिका/kantakari) is the best for relieving cough (कासघ्न/cough reliever).
Lac resin (लाक्षा/shellac) is the best for healing acute wound injuries (क्षतघ्न/wound healer).
Regular intake of Nagabala (नागबला/nagabala) is best for curing chest trauma and phthisis (क्षय क्षत/chest injury relief).
Pushkaramula root (पुष्करमूल/pushkaramula) is best for hiccups, asthma, cough, and flank pain (श्वास कास शूल/respiratory and flank pain reliever).
Goat milk (अजा पयः/goat milk) is best for edema, promoting breast milk, and arresting bleeding (शोफ हर/edema and bleeding reliever).
Clay-clod quenched water (लोष्ट जल/earth-cleared water) is best for severe vomiting and excessive thirst (छर्दि तृष्णा हर/vomiting and thirst reliever).
Bhallataka and Chitraka root (चित्रकमूल/chitraka) are best for relieving dry hemorrhoids (शुष्कार्शः/dry piles reliever).
Kutaja bark (कुटज/kutaja) is the best remedy for bleeding hemorrhoids (रक्तार्शः/bleeding piles reliever).
Puffed rice (लाजा/puffed rice) is the best remedy for controlling vomiting (छर्दिघ्न/emesis stopper).
Barley alkali (यावशूक/barley ash) is best for laxative, digestive, and hemorrhoid relief (पाचनीय अर्शोघ्न/digestive piles remedy).
Regular buttermilk intake (तक्र/buttermilk) is best for piles, edema, IBS, and ghee complications (ग्रहणी अर्शः/IBS and piles remedy).
Regular carnivore meat consumption (कव्याद् मांस/carnivore meat) is best for piles, emaciation, and IBS (शोष ग्रहणी/emaciation and IBS remedy).
Pearl (मुक्ता/pearl) is best among astringent, stomachic, and digestive remedies (संग्रहणीय दीपनीय/astringent and digestive).
Ativisha herb (अतिविषा/ativisha) is best for bowel binding, digestion, and pacifying all humors (सर्वदोषहर/tridosha pacifier).
Bilva fruit (बिल्व/bael fruit) is best for bowel astringency, digestive fire, and vata-kapha pacification (वात कफ हर/vata-kapha pacifier).
Hiberus root (उदीच्य/sugandhabala) is best for cooling, digestion, vomiting, and diarrhea (छर्दि अतीसार/emesis and diarrhea reliever).
Katvanga bark (कट्वंग/aralu) is best for bowel binding and kindling appetite (संग्रहणीय दीपनीय/bowel binder and digestive).
Kutaja bark (कुटज त्वक्/kutaja bark) is best for binding Kapha, Pitta, blood, and drying loose stools (रक्त संग्रहणीय/blood stool binder).
Lotus stamens and Anantamula (अनंता/anantamula) are best for bowel binding and bleeding Pitta (रक्तपित्त हर/bleeding pitta reliever).
Gambhari fruit (काश्मर्य फल/gambhari) is best for arresting bleeding and binding bloody stools (रक्त संग्रहणीय/blood stool binder).
Gandhapriyangu (प्रियंगु/priyangu) is best for arresting severe bleeding attacks (शोणितपित्त हर/severe hemorrhage stopper).
Prishniparni herb (पृश्निपर्णी/prishniparni) is best for blood binding, digestion, vata pacification, and virility (वातहर वृष्य/vata pacifier and aphrodisiac).
Shaliparni herb (शालिपर्णी/shaliparni) is best for virility and pacifying all three humors (सर्वदोषहर/tridosha pacifier).
Bala herb (बला/bala) is best for astringent action, strength building, and vata pacification (बल्य वातहर/strength builder and vata pacifier).
Pippalimula root (पिप्पलीमूल/pippalimula) is best for digestion, appetite, and flatulence relief (दीपनीय आनाहहर/digestive and bloating reliever).
Chitraka root (चित्रकमूल/chitraka) is best for digestion, appetite, and anorectal edema and pain (दीपनीय शूलहर/appetite booster and pain reliever).
Gokshura herb (गोक्षुर/gokshura) is best for dysuria and vata pacification (मूत्रकृच्छ्र हर/dysuria reliever).
Turmeric (हरिद्रा/haldi) is best among anti-diabetic herbal remedies (प्रमेहहर/diabetes reliever).
Bloodletting (रक्तावसेक/bloodletting) is best for abscesses, erysipelas, carbuncles, and scrofula (विद्रधि विसर्प/abscess and erysipelas remedy).
Regular Castor oil intake (एरंड तैल/castor oil) is best for hernia, abdominal tumors, and vata pain (गुल्म शूलहर/hernia and colic reliever).
Garlic (लशुन/garlic) is best for curing abdominal tumors and vata disorders (गुल्म वातहर/tumor and vata reliever).
Asafoetida resin (हिंगु/hing) is best for scraping, digestion, carminative action, and vata-kapha (दीपनीय वातकफहर/carminative and vata-kapha pacifier).
Amlavetasa herb (अम्लवेतस/amlavetasa) is best for laxative, appetite, carminative, and vata-kapha (भेदनीय वातश्लेष्महर/laxative and vata-kapha reliever).
Camel milk (उष्ट्री क्षीर/camel milk) is best for ascites and body edema (उदर शोफघ्न/ascites and edema cure).
Iron ash/powder (अयोरजः/iron rust ash) is best for curing anemia (पांडुरोगघ्न/anemia cure).
Khadira heartwood (खदीर/khair wood) is the best remedy for skin diseases and leprosy (कुष्ठघ्न/skin disease cure).
Vidanga seeds (विडंग/vidanga) is the best remedy for intestinal worms (कृमिघ्न/deworming agent).
Rasna root (रास्ना/rasna) is the best herb for pacifying vata disorders (वातहर/vata pacifier).
Castor root (एरंडमूल/castor root) is best for virility and vata pacification (वृष्य वातहर/aphrodisiac and vata pacifier).
Guggulu resin (गुग्गुलु/guggul) is best for reducing obesity and vata (मेदो वातहर/fat and vata reducer).
Guduchi herb (अमृता/giloy) is best for bowel binding, digestion, vata-kapha, blood, and constipation (दीपनीय वातश्लेष्महर/digestive and vata-kapha pacifier).
Madanaphala fruit (मदनफल/madanaphala) is best for emesis and enema formulations (वमन बस्ति/emetic and enema agent).
Trivrit root (त्रिवृत्/trivrit) is best for easy gentle purgation (सुख विरेचन/easy purgative).
Aragvadha/Chaturangula (चतुरंगुल/amaltas) is best for mild purgation (मृदु विरेचन/mild purgative).
Snuhi latex (स्नुक् पयः/euphorbia milk) is best for drastic strong purgation (तीक्ष्ण विरेचन/drastic purgative).
Apamarga seeds (प्रत्यक्पुष्पी/apamarga) is best for errhine nasal clearance (शिरोविरेचन/nasal errhine).
Triphala (त्रिफला/three fruits) is best for curing cataract and eye diseases (तिमिरघ्न/cataract and eye cure).
Triphala and Guggulu ash (त्रिफला गुग्गुलु/triphala guggulu) is best for obesity and lipoma (मेदोहर/fat reducer).
Shirisha bark (शिरीष/shirisha) is the best herb for counteracting poisons (विषघ्न/antidote).
Amalaki fruit (आमलकी/amla) is the best herb for anti-aging and youth preservation (वयःस्थापन/anti-aging rejuvenator).
Haritaki fruit (हरीतकी/harad) is the best among all wholesome herbs (पथ्य/wholesome herb).
Regular intake of milk and ghee (क्षीर घृत/milk and ghee) is the best rejuvenation therapy (रसायन/rejuvenation).
Roasted flour with equal ghee (सक्तु घृत/flour ghee mix) is best for aphrodisiac and upward vata (वृष्य उदावर्त्तहर/aphrodisiac and udavarta reliever).
Sensual intent and Crocodile semen (संकल्प/romantic mindset) are best among virility boosters (वृष्य/potency enhancer).
Dejection and mental depression (दौर्मनस्य/depression) are worst among virility destroyers (अवृष्य/potency destroyer).
Regular oil gargling (तैल गंडूष/oil gargling) is best for tooth strength and taste (दंत बल/tooth strength).
Sandalwood and Udumbara paste (चंदन उदुंबर/sandalwood paste) are best for cooling anti-inflammatory applications (निर्वापण प्रलेप/cooling paste).
Rasna and Agaru paste (रास्ना अगुरु/rasna agaru) are best for removing coldness (शीतापनयन/cold reliever paste).
Lamajjaka and Usheera paste (उशीर/khus paste) are best for relieving burning, skin flaws, and sweat (दाह स्वेदहर/burning and sweat reliever).
Kushta root (कुष्ठ/kushta) is best for vata-pacifying oil massage and poultices (अभ्यंग उपनाह/oil and poultice).
Yashtimadhu (मधुक/mulethi) is best for eyes, virility, hair, voice, complexion, and wound healing (चक्षुष्य वर्ण्य/vision and complexion booster).
Eating over undigested food (अजीर्णाशन/eating during indigestion) is worst for destroying IBS function (ग्रहणी दूषण/IBS producer).
Eating opposing potency foods (विरुद्ध वीर्य/incompatible potency) is worst for causing dreadful diseases (निंदित व्याधि/dreadful illness producer).
Heavy food intake (गुरु भोजन/heavy food) is worst for causing difficult digestion (दुर्विपाक/difficult digestion).
Overeating (अतिमात्राशन/overeating) is the primary cause of ama toxic indigestion (आमदोष/ama toxicity).
Eating according to digestive fire capacity (यथाग्नि आहार/eating according to agni) is best for kindling metabolic fire (अग्निसंधुक्षण/fire booster).
Adopting habituated diet and lifestyle (सात्म्य आहार/customary lifestyle) is best among healthy habits (सेव्य/healthy practice).
Stable posture during sitting, sleeping, and dining (एकासन/stable posture) is best for comfortable digestion (सुख परिणाम/easy digestion).
Irregular meal timings (विषमाशन/irregular meal) is the main cause of fluctuating digestive fire (अग्निवैषम्य/fluctuating fire).
Timely eating (काल भोजन/timely meal) is best among health-promoting practices (आरोग्यकर/health promoter).
Beautifully presented food (सुदर्शन अन्न/pleasing food) is best for generating appetite and desire (श्रद्धाजनन/appetite generator).
Suppressing natural urges (वेगधारण/suppressing urges) is worst for causing illness (अनारोग्यकर/disease producer).
Satiety and satisfaction (तृप्ति/satiety) is the ultimate result of good food qualities (आहार गुण/food benefit).
Complete starvation (अनशन/starvation) is worst for reducing lifespan (आयुषः ह्रास/lifespan reducer).
Scanty eating and Gavedhuka grain are best for slimming; Uddalaka grain is best for drying (कर्शनीय रूक्षणीय/slimming and drying).
Moderate wine intake (मद्य/wine) is best for generating mental joy and cheerfulness (सौमनस्य/mental joy).
Alcoholic intoxication (मद्याक्षेप/alcohol overdose) is worst for destroying intellect, courage, and memory (धी धृति स्मृतिहर/intellect and memory destroyer).
Excessive sexual intercourse (स्त्री अतिप्रसंग/excessive sex) is the main doorway to emaciation and phthisis (शोष द्वार/emaciation doorway).
Suppressing ejaculatory urge (शुक्रवेग निग्रह/semen urge suppression) is the main cause of impotence (षांढ्यकर/impotence cause).
Foot massage (पाद उद्वर्त्तन/foot massage) is best for stimulating appetite for food (अन्न श्रद्धा/appetite generator).
Seeing slaughterhouses (सूना दर्शन/slaughterhouse view) is worst for causing loss of appetite (अन्नाश्रद्धा/appetite loss).
Perverted sensory use (मिथ्यायोग/perverted use) is the main gateway to disease (व्याधि मुख/disease gateway).
Intercourse during menstruation (रजस्वला गमन/menstrual sex) is the main doorway to misfortune and ill-health (अलक्ष्मी/misfortune cause).
Celibacy and self-control (ब्रह्मचर्य/celibacy) is the best among longevity promoters (आयुष्य/longevity enhancer).
Adultery (परदार गमन/adultery) is worst among lifespan destroyers (अनायुष्य/lifespan reducer).
Overexertion beyond physical capacity (अयथाप्राण आरंभ/overexertion) is worst for obstructing life force (प्राण उपरोध/life force obstructer).
Despondency and grief (विषाद/depression) is the main factor in aggravating disease (रोग वर्द्धन/disease aggravator).
Joy and happiness (हर्ष/joy) is the best for pleasing the body and mind (प्रीणन/refreshing).
Sorrow and grief (शोक/sorrow) is the worst among emaciating factors (शोषक/emaciating factor).
Mental peace and contentment (निर्वृति/tranquility) is best for promoting body nourishment (पुष्टि/nourishment).
Reassurance and encouragement (आश्वास/reassurance) is best for building physical strength (बलकर/strength builder).
Good body nourishment (पुष्टि/nourishment) is best for inducing healthy sleep (स्वप्नकर/sleep producer).
Excessive sleep (स्वप्न/sleep) is the main cause of drowsiness (तंद्राकर/drowsiness cause).
Eating all six tastes (सर्वरस अभ्यास/six tastes diet) is best for building overall strength (बलकर/strength builder).
Relying on a single taste (एकरस अभ्यास/single taste diet) causes weakness, anorexia, and humor aggravation (दौर्बल्य अरुचि/weakness and anorexia).
Fomentation with warm poultices (अभ्याराम/warm poultice) is best for relieving stiffness, cold, colic, and cramps (शीत शूलहर/cold and colic reliever).
Retained dead fetus (गर्भशल्य/retained fetus) is the foremost item requiring surgical extraction (आहार्य/extractable foreign body).
Undigested toxic food in stomach (अजीर्ण/indigested food) is the foremost item requiring emetic extraction (उद्धार्य/extractable stomach content).
Infants and children (बाल/child) are foremost among candidates requiring mild medicines (मृदु भेषज/mild medicine candidate).
Elderly persons (वृद्ध/elderly) are foremost among manageable incurable cases (याप्य/palliative case).
Pregnant women (गर्भिणी/pregnant woman) are foremost among candidates avoiding strong drugs, sex, and exercise (तीक्ष्णौषध वर्जित/drastic drugs forbidden).
Mental happiness (सौमनस्य/mental joy) is the best factor for maintaining pregnancy (गर्भधारण/pregnancy retainer).
Triple humor aggravation (सन्निपात/tridosha outbreak) is the most difficult to treat (दुश्चिकित्स्य/hard to treat).
Ama poison toxicity (आमविष/ama toxicity) is the most incurable condition (अचिकित्स्य/incurable toxicity).
Fever (ज्वर/fever) is the foremost among rapid-onset diseases (शीघ्ररोग/rapid disease).
Skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin disease) are foremost among chronic long-standing diseases (दीर्घरोग/chronic disease).
Tuberculosis (राजयक्ष्मा/tuberculosis) is the foremost among syndrome complexes of multiple diseases (रोगसमूह/syndrome group).
Diabetes (प्रमेह/diabetes) is foremost among persistent stubborn diseases (अनुषंगी/stubborn chronic disease).
Leeches (जलौका/leech) are the best among parasurgical blunt instruments (अनुशस्त्र/parasurgical tool).
Enema apparatus (बस्ति/enema apparatus) is the foremost among therapeutic instruments (यंत्र/therapeutic instrument).
Himalayan mountains (हिमवान्/himalayas) is the best habitat for medicinal herbs (औषधि भूमि/herb habitat).
Soma herb (सोम/soma herb) is the king among all medicinal plants (ओषधि/medicinal herb king).
Arid desert region (मरुभूमि/desert land) is the best healthy geographic land (आरोग्य देश/healthy land).
Marshy land (आनूपभूमि/marshy land) is the worst unwholesome geographic region (अहित देश/unhealthy land).
Strict obedience to physician's orders (निर्देशकारित्व/physician compliance) is the best quality of a patient (आतुर गुण/patient virtue).
Disobedience to medical instructions (अनिर्देशकारित्व/non-compliance) is the foremost fatal sign (रिष्ट/fatal sign).
The physician (भिषक्/physician) is the foremost among the four pillars of medical treatment (चिकित्सांग/medical pillar).
Therapeutic success (सिद्धि/therapeutic success) is the highest credit quality of a physician (वैद्य गुण/physician credit).
Atheist and nihilist (नास्तिक/athiest) is the foremost person to be avoided (वर्ज्य/person to avoid).
Greed and restlessness (लौल्य/greed) is the foremost cause of physical suffering (क्लेशकर/suffering cause).
Self-control (आत्मवत्ता/self-control) is the best quality among benefactors (उपकारी/benefactor virtue).
Scriptural reasoning (शास्त्र तर्क/scriptural logic) is the best instrument for success (साधक/success tool).
Practical experience (दृष्टकर्मता/practical experience) is the best factor for dispelling doubt (निःसंशयकर/doubt dispeller).
Incompetence (असमर्थता/incompetence) is the foremost cause of medical fear (भयकर/fear cause).
Scholarly discussion with medical peers (तद्विद्य संभाषा/peer discussion) is best for expanding intellect (बुद्धिवर्द्धन/intellect expander).
The preceptor teacher (आचार्य/teacher) is the best source for gaining scriptural knowledge (शास्त्राधिगम/scripture learning).
Hatred towards a noble physician (सद्वैद्य द्वेष/physician hatred) is the foremost cause of losing life (प्राणत्याग/life loss cause).
Ayurveda (आयुर्वेद/ayurveda) is the foremost among immortal sciences (अमृत/immortal science).
Truthful words of noble seers (सद्वचन/noble words) are the best instructions to follow (अनुष्ठेय/rule to follow).
Vital air (वायु/vayu) is the chief cause of life force and sensory consciousness (प्राण संज्ञा/life and consciousness).
Renunciation of all worldly attachments (सर्वसंन्यास/total renunciation) is the ultimate source of happiness (सुख/supreme happiness).
Selection of these 155 primary items is determined individually and collectively according to clinical context.
Herbs like Pushkaramula are selected specifically as collective compound formulations.
One hundred and fifty-five primary best items (अग्र्य/primary best) have been stated here in this chapter.
Understand this concise list as an essential guide for determining wholesome vs. unwholesome factors.
Thus ends the thirteenth chapter (त्रयोदशः अध्यायः/thirteenth chapter) named 'Compilation of Primary Best Remedies' (अग्र्यसंग्रहः/primary best remedies) of Ashtanga Sangraha.




Now, therefore, we shall explain the chapter (अध्याय/section) on the compilation of purificatory and therapeutic herb groups (शोधनादिगण/cleansing herb categories).
Fruits, flowers, and leaves of Madanaphala (मदनफल/emetic nut), Jimutaka (जीमूतक/wild luffa), Ikshvaku (इक्ष्वाकु/bitter gourd), and two varieties of Koshataki (कोशातकी/ridge gourd).
Autumn-harvested fruits of Kutaja (कुटज/kurchi bark), Karanja (करंज/Indian beech), Trapusha (त्रपुस/cucumber), Sarshapa (सर्षप/mustard), Pippali (पिप्पली/long pepper), Vidanga (विडंग/false black pepper), Ela (एला/cardamom), Pratyakpushpi (प्रत्यक्पुष्पी/apamarga), Harenu, Prithvika, Kustumburu (कुस्तुम्बुरु/coriander), and Prapunnata.
Roots of Hastiparni, Kovidara, Arishta, Ashwagandha (अश्वगंधा/winter cherry), Vacha (वचा/sweet flag), Chitraka (चित्रक/leadwort), Aragvadha, Patha, Patali, Madhuka, Murva, Shigru (शिग्रु/drumstick), Yavani (यवानी/carom seeds), Punarnava (पुनर्नवा/hogweed), Pippalimula (पिप्पलीमूल/long pepper root), Chavya, Usheera (उशीरा/vetiver), and Hribera.
Exudates and sticky gums (पिच्छा/mucilaginous sap) of Shalmali (शाल्मली/silk cotton tree), Bhadraparni, Upodika, Rasanjana (रसांजन/barberry extract), Rajadana, and Shringataka (शृंगाटक/water chestnut).
Flowers of Priyangu (प्रियंगु/perfumed flower).
Leaves of Talisapatra (तालीसपत्र/fir leaves).
Rhizomes and tubers of Haridra (हरिद्रा/turmeric) and Shringavera (अदरक/fresh ginger).
Heartwood extracts of Madhuka (यष्टिमधु/liquorice) and Daruharidra (दारुहरिद्रा/tree turmeric).
Tagara, Guduchi (गुडूची/giloy), honey, jaggery, plant milk, mineral ashes (क्षार/alkali), and salts—these herbs constitute emetic drugs (वमन द्रव्य/emetic agents).
Roots of Trivrit (त्रिवृत्/Indian jalap), Shyama, Danti (दंती/danti root), Dravanti, Shankhini, Vacha, Swarnakshiri, Chitraka, Laghu-Panchamula, Punarnava, Vastuka, and Shala.
Barks of Tilvaka (तिलवक/lodhra bark), Kampillaka, and Patali.
Fruits of Triphala (त्रिफला/three myrobalans), Peelu, Priyala, Badara, Kashmarya, Parushaka, Draksha (द्राक्षा/raisins), Nilinee, Vidanga, and Eranda (अंडी/castor).
Fruits and leaves of Chaturangula/Aragvadha (अमलतास/golden shower).
Bark, fruits, and leaves of Pootika/Karanja (करंज/Indian beech).
Latex and milky sap (क्षीर/milky sap) of Snuhi (महावृक्ष/euphorbia sap), Saptaparna, and Jyotishmati (ज्योतिष्मती/celastrus).
Milk (क्षीर/milk), whey (मस्तु/whey), wine (मद्य/wine), fermented rice gruel (धान्याम्ल/sour gruel), and animal urines (मूत्र/urine)—these constitute purgative agents (विरेचन द्रव्य/purgative drugs).
Fresh juices of Koshataki, Devadali, Karavella (करेला/bitter gourd), Arka sap (अर्क क्षीर/calotropis latex), and hot water act as dual-action emetic-purgatives (उभयभाग हर/dual evacuant).
Herbs used in enema therapies (बस्ति/enema therapy) across clinical stages are innumerable and categorized as per condition.
Herbs should be selected from taste groups (रस स्कन्ध/taste categories) according to humor imbalance (दोष/humors) and stage of disease.
Fruits commonly used for enemas include Madanaphala, Kutaja, Jimutaka, Ikshvaku, Koshataki, Trapusha, Mustard (सर्षप/mustard), and Shatahva (सौंफ/dill seeds).
Roots of Bala, Dashamula (दशमूल/ten roots), Eranda (अंडी/castor root), Trivrit, Vacha, Yashtimadhu (मुलेठी/liquorice), Kushta, Rasna, and Punarnava.
Devadaru, Hapusha, Hingu, Rasanjana, Trikatu, Ela, Guduchi, Barley, Kulattha, Jaggery, Salt, Whey, Fermented gruel, Urine, Oils, Honey, and Milk—these form non-unctuous decoction enemas (निरूह बस्ति/decoction enema).
Fruits of Apamarga, Vidanga, Black pepper (मरिच/black pepper), Pippali, Shirisha, Bilva, Jeeraka, Ajamoda, Vartaka, and Ela used for head purgation (नस्य/nasal administration).
Leaves of Naleesa, Tamala, Tarkari, and green leafy herbs (हरितक वर्ग/green herbs).
Fruits and leaves of Sarshapa (सर्षप/mustard).
Fruits, leaves, and bark of Shigru (सहजन/drumstick).
Rhizomes, tubers, and leaves of Haridra (हल्दी/turmeric), Radish (मूलक/radish), Garlic (लशुन/garlic), and Ginger (अदरक/ginger).
Tubers of Ativisha (अतिविषा/indian atees).
Roots of Kushta, Vacha, Bharangi, Shweta, Kinihi, Nagadanti, Jyotishmati, Gavakshi, Vayastha, Ashvakali, Bimba, and Karanja.
Flowers and roots of Arka (मदार/calotropis) and Alarka.
Seeds of Rodhra (लोध्र/lodhra bark), Madanaphala, Saptaparna, Nimba (नीम/neem), and Peelu.
Flowers of Murangi, Matulunga (बिजौरा नींबू/citron), and Lavanga (लौंग/clove).
Resins (निर्यास/plant resins) of Aguru, Suradaru, Sarala, Shallaki (शलकी/frankincense), Asana, Rasanjana, Hingu, and Laksha.
Heartwood extracts (सार/heartwood extract) of Tamala, Shala, Tala, Madhuka, and Daruharidra.
Barks of Tejasvini, Meshashringi, Ingudi, and Brihati-dvaya (कंटेरी/nightshade).
Rajadana pulp, honey, salts, wines, cow dung juice (गोमय रस/cow dung juice), animal urine, bile (पित्त/bile), unctuous oils, milk, blood, meat broth, grain water, and water—these serve as head errhines (शिरोविरेचन/nasal errhines).
Madhuka, Padmaka, Manjistha, Sariva, Musta, Punnaga, Elavaluka, Cinnamon bark, Shatapushpa, Shallaki, Sugar, Banyan, Udumbara, Ashwattha, Plaksha, Lodhra, Lotus flowers, and aromatic drugs (except Kushta and Tagara) form daily medicinal smoke (प्रायोगिक धूम/daily smoke therapy).
Aguru, Guggulu, Shallaki, Shaileya, Usheera, Musta, Cinnamon, Kunduru, Resin, Beeswax (मधूच्छिष्ट/beeswax), Bilva pulp, Barley, Sesame, Black gram, Saffron, Fats (वसा/fats), and Ghee form unctuous smoking (स्नैहिक धूम/unctuous smoke therapy).
Head evacuative drugs, strong aromatic substances, Manahshila (मैनसिल/realgar), and Haritala (हरताल/orpiment) form sharp unctuous smoke (तीक्ष्ण धूम/sharp smoke therapy).
Bhadradaru, Tagara, Varuna, Bala, Atibala, Bharangi, Vidaryadi group, and six Panchamulas (excluding grass roots) constitute Vata-pacifying herbs (वातशमन/vata pacifier).
Durva, Ananta, Mocharasa, Manjistha, Date, Raisins (द्राक्षा/raisins), Shatavari, Nagakeshara, Priyangu, Dhataki, Khadira, Lotus varieties, cooling potency herbs (शीत वीर्य/cold potency), Nyagrodhadi group, and Trina-panchamula constitute Pitta-pacifying herbs (पित्तशमन/pitta pacifier).
Shatapushpa, Devadaru, Langali, Aragvadhadi group, Arkadi group, Surasadi group, Mushkakadi group, Vatsakadi group, Mustadi group, Valli-panchamula, and Kantaka-panchamula constitute Kapha-pacifying herbs (कफशमन/kapha pacifier).
Here occurs a concluding verse (श्लोक/concluding verse).
Having thoroughly examined humors (दोष/humors), tissues (दूष्य/tissue elements), and habitat, these well-administered herb groups effectively cure all diseases (सर्व रोग नाशन/cure of all diseases).
For self-controlled patients, these therapeutic herb groups consistently achieve complete cure (रोग शान्ति/complete disease cure).
Thus ends the fourteenth chapter (चतुर्दशः अध्यायः/fourteenth chapter) named 'Purificatory and Therapeutic Herb Groups' (शोधनादिगणसंग्रहः/cleansing herb categories) of Ashtanga Sangraha.




Now, therefore, we shall explain the chapter (अध्याय/section) on the compilation of major decoctions (महाकषाय/fifty major decoction groups).
Owing to the endless variations of diseases (आमय/diseases) and drugs (औषध/medicinal plants), decoction combinations are infinite (अनन्त/endless).
Those decoctions of high utility and supreme efficacy are categorized into 45 major groups (महाकषाय/major decoctions) starting with Jivaniya, each containing ten herbs (दशकषाय/ten herb groups).
These group classifications are stated for the clear understanding of beginners (मन्दबुद्धि/novice students) and as illustrative examples for wise physicians (बुद्धिमान्/scholars).
Intelligent physicians, seeing sweet, cold, and unctuous properties (गुण/attributes) in Jivanti, can similarly infer Jivaniya action in milk, sugarcane, grapes, walnut, and Vidarikanda.
However, for beginners (मन्दबुद्धि/ordinary learners), following the exact stated herb combinations (यथोक्त/as explicitly instructed) is preferable.
Individually enumerated, these herbs across the groups total four hundred and fifty medicinal drugs (चार सौ पचास/450 herbs) in title.
Even though the same herbs overlap across multiple groups (द्रव्यसंकर/herb overlap), there is no conflict in group nomenclature.
Because a single herb (एक द्रव्य/single drug) is capable of performing multiple therapeutic actions (बहुकार्य/diverse actions).
Excluding salt taste (लवण रस/salty taste), the remaining five tastes are called (कषाय/decoctions) (कषाय/pharmaceutical decoctions) in pharmaceutical preparations.
Because those five tastes form the base origin (यौनि/fundamental source) for pharmaceutical extract preparations.
In salt, pharmaceutical forms like resinous extracts, decoctions, and pastes (कषाय/astringent taste) cannot be prepared.
Moreover, salt lacks independent decoction utility (नैरर्थक्य/meaningless in extract form), making salt decoction preparations unviable.
Verse summary on the fifty major decoction groups follows:
Jivanti, Kakoli, Ksheerakakoli, Meda, Mahameda, Mudgaparni, Mashaparni,
Rishabhaka, Jeevaka, and Yashtimadhu constitute the life-promoting group (जीवनीय/vitalizer group).
Vatya, Bala, Ksheeravidari, Kakoli, Ksheerakakoli, Ikshu, Ashwagandha,
Ksheerini, Rajakshavaka, and Ksharadvaji form the body-nourishing group (बृंहणीय/weight-promoting group).
Haimavati, Chirabilva, Musta, Kushtha, Vacha, Haridra, Daruharidra,
Chitraka, Katuki, and Ativisha constitute the tissue-scraping group (लेखनीय/tissue-reducing group).
Arka, Eranda, Chitra, Chitraka, Chirabilva, Shankhini, Sarala,
Hemakshiri, Katuki, and Langali form the purgative-purgation group (भेदनीय/purgative evacuants).
Honey, Yashtimadhu, Prishniparni, Katphala, Lodhra, Priyangu, Dhataki,
Ambashtaki, Samanga, and Mocharasa constitute the fracture-healing group (संधान/tissue-union group).
Hing, Maricha, Amlavetasa, Yavani, Bhallataka seed nut,
Along with Panchakola herbs, forms the digestive-fire kindling group (दीपनीय/appetite-booster group).
Aindri, Atirasa, Ksheeravidari, RishyaproKta, Shaliparni, Bala, Atibala,
Ashwagandha, Katuki, and Rishabhaka make up the strength-promoting group (बल्य/strength enhancer group).
Chandana, Punnaga, Ksheeravidari, White sugar, Shveta-sariva, Yashtimadhu, Padmaka, Usheera,
Manjishta, and Krishna-sariva constitute the complexion-enhancing group (वर्ण्य/complexion booster group).
Hansapadi, Brihati, Kantakari, Draksha, Sariva, Ikshumula,
Katphala, Yashtimadhu, Pippali, and Vidarikanda form the voice-improving group (कंठ्य/voice-clearing group).
Vrikshamla, Badara, Dadima, Kuvala, Amra, Amrataka, Lakucha, Karamarda,
Matulunga, and Amlavetasa constitute the heart-pleasing group (हृद्य/cardiac toner group).
Shunthi, Chavya, Chitraka, Vidanga, Murva, Guduchi, Vacha, Musta,
Pippali, and Patola form the thirst-relieving group (तृष्णा-निग्रहण/thirst-subduing group).
Kutaja fruit, Bilva, Chitraka, Shunthi, Ativisha, Vacha, Chavya,
Dhanvayasa, Haritaki, and Daruharidra constitute the hemorrhoid-curing group (अर्शोघ्न/piles-curing group).
Khadira, Amalaki, Bhallataka, Haridra, Saptaparna, Karaveera,
Aragvadha, Vidanga, and Jasmine leaves form the skin-disease curing group (कुष्ठघ्न/leprosy-curing group).
Jatamansi, Aragvadha, Chandana, Sarshapa, Musta, Nimba, Kutaja, Yashtimadhu,
Daruharidra, and Karanja constitute the itching-relieving group (कंडूघ्न/itching-destroying group).
Shigru, Maricha, Kembuka, Vidanga, Kandira, Achyranthes, Nirgundi,
Gokshura, Ativisha, and Musakaparni form the worm-destroying group (कृमिघ्न/anti-parasitic group).
Manjishta, Shleshmataka, Haridra, Rasna, Shirisha, Trivrit,
Ela, Chandana, Kataka, and Nirgundi form the poison-neutralizing group (विषघ्न/antidote group).
Shali rice, Kusha, Kasha, Shashtika rice, Veerana, Darbha, Ikshu, Balikshu,
Gundra, and Utkata roots constitute the lactation-promoting group (स्तन्यजनन/galactagogue group).
Patha, Shunthi, Devadaru, Musta, Guduchi, Sariva, Indrajava, Murva,
Katuki, and Kiratatikta form the breastmilk-purifying group (स्तन्यशोधन/milk-cleansing group).
Meda, Kakoli, Ksheerakakoli, Vandaka, Jeevaka, Rishabhaka, Kulinga,
Jatamansi, Mudgaparni, and Mashaparni constitute the semen-promoting group (शुक्रजनन/spermatogenic group).
Kushtha, Elavaluka, Katphala, Kanda-ikshu, Samudraphena, Usheera,
Vasuka, Ikshuraka, and Kadamba resin form the semen-purifying group (शुक्रशोधन/semen-cleansing group).
Draksha, Kakoli, Ksheerakakoli, Guduchi, Yashtimadhu, Jeevaka, Vidarikanda,
Meda, Jivanti, and Shaliparni form the unction-assisting group (स्नेहोपग/oleation-booster group).
Shigru, Punarnava, Shveta-sariva, Krishna-sariva, Kulattha, Masha, Badara,
Yava, Tila, Arka, and Eranda constitute the sudation-assisting group (स्वेदोपग/fomentation-booster group).
Laja, Amra, Badara, Dadima, Yava, Shashtika rice, Matulunga, Usheera,
Jambu leaves, Amra leaves, and pure mud form the emesis-checking group (वमन-निग्रहण/anti-emetic group).
Shunthi, Dhanvayasa, Hiberam, Parpataka, Chandana, Guduchi,
Bhunimba, Musta, Patola, and Dhanyaka constitute the feverish-thirst relieving group (तृष्णा-शमन/thirst-pacifier group).
Brihati, Kantakari, Vandaka, Pushkaramula, Haritaki, Pippali, Karkatashringi,
Shati, Duralabha, and Badara seeds form the hiccup-relieving group (हिक्का-निग्रहण/anti-hiccup group).
Shyama, Anantamula, Padmaka, Katvanga, Padmakeshara, Lodhra,
Dhataki flower, Samanga, and Mocharasa form the bowel-binding group (पुरीष-संग्रहणीय/astringent bowel-binder group).
Jambu, Shallaki, Yashtimadhu, Nilotpala, Kacchura, Krishna-sariva,
Pure earth, Ksheeravidari, and Shalmali form the stool-coloring group (पुरीष-विरजनीय/stool-pigment altering group).
Jambu, Amra, Udumbara, Vata, Kapitana, Plaksha, Pippala, Ashmantaka,
Bhallataka, and Somavalka form the urine-reducing group (मूत्र-संग्रहणीय/antidiuretic group).
Kamala, Nalin, Kumuda, Yashtimadhu, Saugandhika, Dhataki, Priyangu flower,
Utpala, Shatapatra, and Pundarika constitute the urine-color altering group (मूत्र-विरजनीय/urine-pigment altering group).
Vrikshadani, Gokshura, Darbha, Utkata, Vasuka, Pashanabheda, Kusha, Kasha,
Gundra, and Erandamula form the diuretic urine-evacuating group (मूत्र-विरेचनीय/diuretic evacuant group).
Draksha, Amalaki, Punarnava, Shveta-sariva, Krishna-sariva, Duralabha, Haritaki, Pippali,
Karkatashringi, Tamalaki, and Kantakari form the cough-curing group (कासहर/anti-tussive group).
Chanda, Amlavetasa, Shati, Tamalaki, Tulasi, Hingu, Jivanti,
Pushkaramula, Ela, and Agaru constitute the dyspnea-relieving group (श्वासहर/anti-asthmatic group).
Draksha, Peelu, Parushaka, Manjishta, Sariva, Guduchi, Patha,
And Triphala herbs form the fever-curing group (ज्वरहर/anti-pyretic group).
Dadima, Falgula, Parushaka, Priyala, Yava, Shashtika rice, Ikshu, Badara,
Draksha, and Kharjura constitute the fatigue-relieving group (श्रमहर/fatigue-dispelling group).
Padmaka, Laja, Usheera, Yashtimadhu, Utpala, Sariva, White sugar, Hiberam,
Kashmarya fruit, and Chandana form the burning-sensation relieving group (दाह-प्रशमन/anti-burning group).
Tagara, Shunthi, Agaru, Vacha, Dhanyaka, Bhutika, Pippali,
Pippalimula, Shyonaka, and Agnimantha form the coldness-dispelling group (शीत-प्रशमन/anti-cold group).
Tinduka, Priyala, Bijaka, Saptaparna, Khadira, Kadara, Badara,
Arimeda, Aswakarna, and Arjuna constitute the urticaria-relieving group (उदर्द-प्रशमन/anti-urticaria group).
Kakoli, Ela, Usheera, Kantakari, Shaliparni, Prishniparni,
Chandana, Yashtimadhu, and Eranda form the body-ache relieving group (अंगमर्द-प्रशमन/anti-bodyache group).
Yavani, Maricha, Ajaji, Gandira, Ajagandha,
Along with Panchakola herbs, forms the colic-relieving group (शूल-प्रशमन/anti-colic group).
Honey, Yashtimadhu, Laja, Gairika, Priyangu, Mocharasa, Earthen pot-sherds,
Lodhra with sugar, constitute the hemorrhage-checking group (शोणित-स्थापन/hemostatic group).
Silajatu, Elavaluka, Katphala, Mocharasa, Ashoka, Padmaka, Shirisha,
Punnaga, Kadamba, and Vetasa form the pain-alleviating group (वेदना-स्थापन/analgesic group).
Katphala, Hingu, Choraka, Guggulu, Ashokarohini, Vayastha,
Arimeda, Jatamansi, Golomi, and Vacha constitute the consciousness-restoring group (संज्ञा-स्थापन/resuscitative group).
Aindri, Durva, Abhaya, Vishvaksena, Haritaki, Arishta,
Brahmi, Vatyapushpi, and Shatavari constitute the pregnancy-stabilizing group (गर्भ-स्थापन/fetal-promoter group).
Guduchi, Haritaki, Amalaki, Jivanti, Gaja-pippali, Shaliparni,
Mandukaparni, Atirasa, and Punarnava form the youth-preserving group (वयः-स्थापन/anti-aging group).
Thus, these 45 major decoction groups (महाकषाय/major decoctions) are illustrated to combat diverse diseases (नानाविध व्याधि/various ailments).
The wise physician should formulate and adapt them according to specific disease states, stage, and clinical requirements.
Thus ends the fifteenth chapter (पञ्चदशः अध्यायः/fifteenth chapter) named 'Compilation of Major Decoctions' (महाकषायसंग्रहः/fifty major decoctions) of Ashtanga Sangraha.




Now, therefore, we shall explain the chapter (अध्याय/section) on the collection of various herb groups (विविध गण/diverse plant groups) of Ashtanga Sangraha.
Vidari (विदारी/kudzu tuber), Panchangula (पंचांगुल/castor), Vrishchikali (वृश्चिकाली/stinging nettle), Vrishchiva (वृश्चीव/white punarnava), Devahvaya (देवदारु/deodar cedar), Shurpaparni (शूर्पपर्णी/mudgaparni).
Two Panchamula groups (पंचमूल/five roots), Laghu-panchamula and Jivana-panchamula, Kandukari (कंडूकरी/cowhage), Gopasuta (गोपसुता/sariva), Tripadi (त्रिपादी/three-footed herb).
This Vidaryadi group (विदार्यादि गण/vidari herb group) is pleasing to the heart (हृद्य/cardiac tonic), nourishing (बृंहण/body builder), and pacifies vata and pitta humors (वात पित्त शमन/vata pitta reducer).
This herb group cures wasting disease (शोष/emaciation), abdominal tumors (गुल्म/phantom tumor), bodyache (अंगमर्द/body pain), dyspnea (श्वास/asthma), and cough (कास/cough).
Sariva (सारिवा/indian sarsaparilla), Usheera (उशीर/khus vetiver), Kashmarya (काश्मर्य/gambhari fruit), Yashtimadhu (मधुक/licorice root), and two cold herbs (शिशिर द्वय/cooling duo).
Yashtimadhu (यष्टी/licorice) and Parushaka (परूषक/phalsa fruit) cure burning sensation (दाह/burning heat), bleeding pitta (पित्तास्त्र/bleeding disorder), and excessive thirst (तृष्णा/thirst).
Padmaka (पद्मक/wild cherry), Pundra (पुंडरीक/lotus stem), Vriddhi (वृद्धि/growth tuber), Tugaksheeri (तुगाक्षीरी/bamboo manna), Parpataka (पर्पट/fumitory), Shringi (शृंगी/gall nut), Guduchi (अमृता/giloy)—these ten herbs are named Jivaniya group (जीवनीय/vitality group).
They promote breast milk production (स्तन्यजनन/lactation booster), cure vata and pitta disorders (वात पित्त शमन/vata pitta cure), and provide gratification, body nourishment, and longevity (बृंहण जीवन/nourishment and vital growth).
Parushaka (परूषक/phalsa), Triphala Vara (वरा/triphala), Draksha (द्राक्षा/raisins), Katphala (कट्फल/box myrtle), and Kataka fruit (कतक फल/clearing nut).
Rajavriksha (राजवृक्ष/golden shower), Dadima (दाडिम/pomegranate), and leafy greens cure thirst (तृष्णा/thirst), urinary disorders (मूत्रामय/urinary diseases), and vata humor (वात/wind humor).
Anjana (अंजन/antimony), Phalini (फलिनी/priyangu), Jatamansi (मांसी/spikenard), Padma lotus (पद्म/lotus), Utpala (उत्पल/blue waterlily), and Rasanjana (रसांजन/berberis extract).
Ela (एला/cardamom), Yashtimadhu (मधुक/licorice), Nagakeshara (नागकेशर/ironwood flower) cure poisons (विष/toxins), internal burning heat (अन्तर्दाह/inner burning), and pitta humor (पित्त/bile humor).
Patola (पटोल/pointed gourd), Katuki (कटुरोहिणी/picrorhiza), Chandana (चंदन/sandalwood), Madhusrava (मधुस्त्रव/sweet resin), Guduchi (गुडूची/giloy), along with Patha (पाठा/velvet leaf).
Cures kapha-pitta humors (कफ पित्त/kapha pitta), skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin disorders), fever (ज्वर/fever), poisons (विष/toxins), vomiting (वमि/emesis), anorexia (अरोचक/loss of appetite), and jaundice (कामला/jaundice).
Guduchi (गुडूची/giloy), Padmaka (पद्मक/cherrywood), Nimba Arishta (अरिष्ट/neem), Dhanyaka (धान्यक/coriander), and Red Sandalwood (रक्तचंदन/red sandalwood).
Cures pitta-kapha fevers (पित्त कफ ज्वर/pitta kapha fever), vomiting (छर्दि/vomiting), burning heat (दाह/burning sensation), thirst (तृष्णा/thirst), and kindles digestive fire (अग्निकृत्/appetite booster).
Aragvadha (आरग्वध/golden shower), Indrayava (इंद्रयव/conessi seeds), Patalika (पाटला/trumpet flower), Nimba (निंब/neem), Guduchi (अमृता/giloy), Madhurasa (मधुरसा/murcha), Vikankata (स्रुववृक्ष/flacourtia), Patha (पाठा/velvet leaf).
Bhunimba (भूनिंब/kalmegh), Sairyaka (सैर्यक/barleria), Patola (पटोल/pointed gourd), two Karanjas (करंज द्वय/two pongamia species), Saptaparna (समच्छद/dita bark), Chitraka (अग्नि/leadwort), Sushavi (सुषवी/black cumin), Bana (बाण/barleria shoot), Ghonta (घोंटा/jujube variety).
This Aragvadhadi group (आरग्वधादि गण/aragvadha group) overcomes vomiting (छर्दि/vomiting), skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin disorders), poisons (विष/toxins), and fever (ज्वर/fever).
It destroys kapha humor (कफ/phlegm humor), itching (कंडू/itching), diabetes (प्रमेह/urinary disorders), and cleanses septic stubborn ulcers (दुष्ट व्रण/septic wounds).
Asana (असन/vijaysar), Tinisha (तिनिश/sandan wood), Bhurja (भूर्ज/birch bark), Arjun Shwetavaha (श्वेतवाह/arjuna tree), Prakirya (प्रकीर्या/karanja variety), Khadira (खदिर/catechu), Kadara (कदर/white catechu), Bhandi (भंडी/sirisha), Shimshapa (शिंशपा/rosewood), Meshashringi (मेषशृंगी/gymnema).
Three cooling herbs (त्रिहिम/three cooling woods), Palasha (पलाश/flame of forest), Jongaka (जोंगक/aloe-wood), Shaka (शाक/teak), Shala (शाल/sal tree), Dhava (धव/axlewood), Kramuka (क्रमुक/areca nut), Kalinga (कलिंग/conessi bark), Chhagalakarna (छागकर्ण/sal variety), Ashvakarna (अश्वकर्ण/sal tree).
This Asanadi group (असनादि गण/asana group) conquers leucoderma (श्वित्र/vitiligo), skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin disorders), excess kapha (कफ/phlegm), and intestinal worms (कृमि/parasites).
It destroys anemia (पांडुरोग/anemia), diabetes (प्रमेह/urinary sugar), and fatty tissue disorders (मेदोदोष/obesity disorders).
Varana (वरण/crataeva bark), two Sairyakas (सैर्यक द्वय/two barleria species), Shatavari (शतावरी/asparagus root), Chitraka Dahana (दहन/leadwort), Morata (मोरट/root of sugarcane), Bilva (बिल्व/bael fruit), Vishanika (विषाणिका/horn-shaped pod).
Two Brihatis (बृहती द्वय/two nightshades), two Karanjas (करंज द्वय/two pongamia varieties), two Jayas (जया द्वय/two premna species), Bahalapallava (बहलपल्लव/thick leaves), Darbha grass (दर्भ/sacred grass), Rujakara (रुजाकर/pain-producing herb).
This Varanadi group (वरणादि गण/varana group) controls excess kapha (कफ/phlegm), body fat (मेदः/fat tissue), and sluggish digestion (मंदाग्नि/low digestive fire).
Cures stiffness of thighs (आढ्यवात/gouty arthritis), headache (शिरःशूल/head pain), abdominal tumors (गुल्म/phantom tumor), and internal deep abscesses (विद्रधि/internal abscess).
Tutthaka (तुत्थक/blue vitriol), Hingu (हिंगु/asafoetida), two types of Kaseesa (कासीस द्वय/green vitriol varieties), Saindhava rock salt (सैंधव/rock salt).
Along with Shilajit (शिलाजतु/mineral pitch), it cures dysuria (मूत्रकृच्छ्र/painful urination), urinary calculi (अश्मरी/urinary calculi), abdominal tumor (गुल्म/phantom tumor/mass), excess fat (मेदः/fat tissue), and kapha.
Veeratara (वीरतर/dichrostachys bush), Aranika (अरणिक/premna root), Nala grass (नल/reed grass), Guntha (गुंठ/root variety), Morata (मोरट/sweet root), Tuntuka (टुंटुक/shyonaka), two Sairyakas (सैर्यक द्वय/barleria duo).
Mustaka (मुस्तक/nut grass), Manjari (मंजरी/flower cluster), Karkasha (कर्कश/rough leaf), Partha Arjuna (पार्थ/arjuna bark)—these ten herbs form the diuretic group (मूत्रविरेचन गण/diuretic class).
This Veeratiradi group (वीरतरादि गण/veeratara group) destroys all diseases caused by aggravated vata humor (वात व्याधि/vata disorders).
Cures urinary stones (अश्मरी/kidney stone), gravel in urine (शर्करा/urinary gravel), dysuria (मूत्रकृच्छ्र/painful urination), urinary retention (मूत्राघात/urinary blockage), and pain.
Lodhra (रोध्र/symplocos bark), Shabaraka-lodhra (शाबरक रोध्र/white lodhra), Palasha (पलाश/flame of forest), Jingini (जिंगिणी/gum tree), Sarala (सरल/pine wood), Katphala (कट्फल/box myrtle).
Kutsitamba (कुत्सितांब/bitter mango), Kadali (कदली/banana stem), Ashoka (अशोक/ashoka tree), Elavaluka (एलवालुक/red cherry seed), Paripelava (परिपेल्व/cyperus water grass), Mocha (मोच/silk cotton resin).
This Lodhradi group (रोध्रादि गण/lodhra group) destroys body fat (मेदः/fat tissue) and kapha humor (कफ/phlegm).
Cures uterine and vaginal disorders (योनिदोष/gynecological disorders), acts as astringent (स्तंभी/hemostatic binder), brightens complexion (वर्ण्य/skin illuminator), and destroys poisons (विष/toxins).
Arka (अर्क/calotropis), Alka (अल्क/white calotropis), Nagadanti (नागदंती/wild castor), Vishalya (विशल्या/gloriosa lily), Bharangi (भार्ङ्गी/pygmy flower), Rasna (रास्ना/pluchea bark), Vrishchikali (वृश्चिकाली/stinging herb), Prakirya (प्रकीर्या/karanja).
Pratyakpushpi (प्रत्यक्पुष्पी/apamarga), Peetataila (पीततैल/jyotishmati oil seed), Odakeerya (उदकीर्या/karanja), two Shwetas (श्वेता द्वय/two white flowered roots), Tapasa-vriksha (तापस वृक्ष/ingudi tree).
This Arkadi group (अर्कादि गण/arka group) removes excess kapha (कफ/phlegm), fat (मेदः/fat tissue), and poisons (विष/toxins).
It pacifies intestinal worms (कृमि/parasites), skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin disorders), and specifically purifies septic ulcers (व्रणशोधन/wound cleanser).
Two Surasas (सुरस युग/tulsi varieties), Phanijjaka (फणिज्जक/wild basil), Kalamala (कालमाला/black basil), Vidanga (विडंग/false black pepper), Kharabuka (खरबुक/rough leaf), Vrishakarni (वृषकर्णी/mouse-ear herb), Katphala (कट्फल/box myrtle), Kasamarda (कासमर्द/negro coffee bush).
Kshavaka (क्षवक/sneezewort), Sarasi (सरसि/water lily), Bharangi (भार्ङ्गी/pygmy flower), Karmuka (कार्मुक/bamboo shoot), Kakamachi (काकमाची/black nightshade), Kulahala (कुलहल/celsia herb), Vishamushti (विषमुष्टी/nux vomica), Bhustrina (भूस्तृण/lemon grass), Bhutakeshi (भूतकेशी/spikenard variety).
This Surasadi group (सुरसादि गण/surasa basil group) destroys phlegm (श्लेष्मा/kapha), body fat (मेदः/fat tissue), and worms (कृमि/parasites).
Cures rhinitis (प्रतिश्याय/common cold), anorexia (अरोचक/loss of appetite), dyspnea (श्वास/asthma), cough (कास/cough), and purifies wounds (व्रणशोधन/wound cleanser).
Mushkaka (मुष्कक/tree ash), Dhruba (ध्रुव/stable wood), Varahi (वाराही/yam tuber), Pippali (पिप्पली/long pepper), Palasha (पलाश/flame of forest), Dhava (धव/axlewood), Shimshapa (शिंशपा/rosewood).
Cures abdominal tumors (गुल्म/phantom tumor), diabetes (प्रमेह/urinary sugar), kidney stones (अश्मरी/calculi), anemia (पांडु/anemia), obesity (मेदः/fat), piles (अर्शः/hemorrhoids), kapha, and semen defects (शुक्रदोष/semen disorders).
Vatsaka (वत्सक/kutaja bark), Madhurasa (मधुरसा/murcha), Truti Ela (त्रुटि/cardamom), Vacha (वचा/sweet flag), Dirghavrinta Shyonaka (दीर्घवृंत/shyonaka), Velva (वेल्ल/vidanga), Sarshapa (सर्षप/mustard).
Katurohini (रोहिणी/picrorhiza), Sthapani (स्थपनि/herbal anchor), Hingu (हिंगु/asafoetida), Bharangi (भार्ङ्गी/pygmy flower), Shailaghati Pashanabheda (शैलघाति/stone breaker), Ghanapriya (घनप्रिय/nut grass lover).
This Vatsakadi group (वत्साकादि गण/kutaja group) conquers vata and kapha humors (अनिल श्लेष्मा/vata kapha), excess fat (मेदः/fat tissue), anorexia (अरोचक/loss of appetite), and rhinitis (पीनस/catarrh).
Cures colic pain (शूल/abdominal colic), piles (अर्शः/hemorrhoids), fever (ज्वर/fever), abdominal tumor (गुल्म/phantom tumor/mass), and acts as appetite booster and digestive (दीपन पाचन/digestive fire stimulant).
Vacha (वचा/sweet flag), Jalada Musta (जलद/nut grass), Devadaru (देवाह्व/deodar cedar), Nagara Ginger (नागर/dry ginger), Ativisha (अतिविषा/aconite root), Abhaya Haritaki (अभया/myrobalan).
Two Turmerics (हरिद्रा द्वय/turmeric and tree turmeric), Yashtimadhu (यष्ट्याह्व/licorice), Kalashi (कलशी/prishniparni), Kutaja (कुटज/conessi bark).
Vchadi and Haridradi groups (वचा हरिद्रादि गण/vacha and haridra groups) digest undigested food toxins (आम/undigested chyme) and cure diarrhea (अतिसार/diarrhea).
They destroy body fat (मेदः/fat tissue), excess kapha (कफ/phlegm), severe vata (आढ्यवात/gouty arthritis), and breast milk impurities (स्तन्यदोष/milk toxicity).
Priyangu flowers (प्रियंगु/fragrant berry), two Anjanas (अंजन युग्म/antimony and berberis extract), Padma (पद्म/lotus), lotus pollen (पद्म रज/lotus pollen), Yojanavalli Manjistha (योजनवल्ली/madder root), Ananta (अनंता/sarsaparilla).
Manadruma (मानद्रुम/large tree), Mocharasa (मोचरस/silk cotton gum), Samanga (समंगा/touch-me-not), Punnaga (पुन्नाग/calophyllum), Sheeta (शीत/cooling herb), Madaniya-hetu (मदनीय/intoxicating wood).
Ambashta (अंबष्ठा/velvet leaf), Yashtimadhu (मधुक/licorice), Namaskari (नमस्करी/sensitive plant), Nandivriksha (नंदीवृक्ष/ficus tree), Palasha (पलाश/flame of forest), Kachchura (कच्छुरा/cowhage).
Lodhra (रोध्र/symplocos bark), Dhataki flowers (धातकि/fire-flame bush), Bilva pulp (बिल्व पेशिका/unripe bael fruit), Katvanga (कट्फंग/shyonaka), lotus pollen (कमल रज/lotus pollen).
Priyangvadi and Ambashtadi groups (प्रियंगु अंबष्ठादि गण/priyangu ambashta groups) cure chronic dysentery and diarrhea (पक्वातीसार/matured diarrhea).
They act as bone and tissue healers (संधानीय/tissue binder), benefit pitta disorders (पित्त शमन/pitta pacifier), and heal open wounds (व्रणरोपण/wound healer).
Musta (मुस्ता/nut grass), Vacha (वचा/sweet flag), Chitraka Agni (अग्नि/leadwort), two Turmerics (द्विनिशा/turmeric duo), Katuki (तिक्ता/bitter root), Bhallataka (भल्लात/marking nut), Patha (पाठा/velvet leaf), Triphala (त्रिफला/three myrobalans), Ativisha (विषा/aconite root).
Kushtha (कुष्ठ/saussurea root), Truti Ela (त्रुटि/cardamom), Haimavati (हैमवती/white vacha)—these cure uterine diseases (योनि रोग/vaginal disorders), purify breast milk (स्तन्य विशोधन/milk purifier), and digest fecal waste (मलपाचन/waste eliminator).
Nyagrodha (न्यग्रोध/banyan bark), Pippala (पिप्पल/sacred fig), Sadaphala (सदाफल/gular fig), two Lodhras (रौध्र युग्म/two symplocos barks), two Jambus (जंबू द्वय/two jamun trees), Arjuna (अर्जुन/arjuna bark), Kapeetana (कपीतन/hog plum), Somavalka (सोमवल्क/white khadir).
Plaksha (प्लक्ष/fig tree), Amra (आम्र/mango bark), Vanjula (वंजुल/cane stem), Piyala (पियाल/chironji), Palasha (पलाश/dhak tree), Nandi (नंदी/ficus variety), Kadamba (कदंब/burflower tree), Virala (विरला/tendu tree), Madhuka (मधुक/mahua flower), Yashtimadhu (मधुक/licorice).
This Nyagrodhadi group (न्यग्रोधादि गण/banyan group) cleanses wounds (व्रण्य/wound purifier), acts as astringent (संग्राही/bowel binder), and unites fractured bones (भग्नसाधन/bone-fracture healer).
It destroys excess fat (मेदः/obesity), bleeding pitta (पित्तास्र/bleeding disorders), thirst (तृष्णा/thirst), burning sensation (दाह/burning heat), and gynecological diseases (योनि रोग/uterine disorders).
Two Elas (एला युग्म/two cardamoms), Turushka (तुरुष्क/incense resin), Kushtha (कुष्ठ/saussurea), Phalini (फलिनी/priyangu), Jatamansi (मांसी/spikenard), Hribera Jala (जल/khus vetiver), Dhyamaka (ध्यानक/scented grass), Sprikka (स्पृक्का/clover herb), Choraka (चोरक/angelica root), Chocha Patra (चोचपत्र/cinnamon leaf), Tagara (तगर/valerian), Sthauaneya (स्थौणेय/yew leaf), Jati (जाती/jasmine flower), Rasa (रस/myrrh resin).
Shukti (शुक्ति/pearl oyster shell), Vyaghranakha (व्याघ्रनख/tiger claw shell), Devadaru (अमराह्व/deodar cedar), Aguru (अगरु/aloe-wood), Shrivasaka (श्रीवासक/turpentine resin), Kumkuma (कुंकुम/saffron), Chandana (चंदन/sandalwood), Guggulu (गुग्गुलु/bdellium resin), Devadhupa (देवधूप/sal resin), Ravipura (रवपुरा/incense powder), Punnaga (पुन्नाग/calophyllum), Nagakeshara (नागाह्वय/ironwood flower).
This Eladi group (एलादि गण/cardamom group) controls vata and kapha humors (वात कफ/vata kapha) and overcomes poisons (विष/toxins).
It clarifies skin complexion (वर्णप्रसादन/skin illuminator) and destroys itching (कंडू/itching), boils (पिटिका/skin pustules), and allergic skin rashes (कोठ/urticaria).
Shyama (श्यामा/black trivrit), Danti (दंती/wild castor root), Dravanti (द्रवंती/croton root), Kramuka (क्रमुक/betel nut), Kutharana (कुठरणा/purgative bark), Shankhini (शंखिनी/conch herb), Charmasahva (चर्मसाह्व/purgative pod), Swarnakshiri (स्वर्णक्षीरी/yellow thistle), Gavakshi (गवाक्षी/bitter cucumber), Shikhari Apamarga (शिखरि/apamarga), Rajana (रजनी/turmeric), Chhinnaroha Guduchi (छिन्नरोहा/giloy), Karanja (करंज/pongamia).
Bastantri (बस्तांत्री/goat climber), Vyadhighata Aragvadha (व्याधिघात/golden shower), Bahala (बहल/thick bark), Bahurasa (बहुरस/juicy sugarcane), fruits of Snuhi (तीक्ष्णवृक्ष फल/purging milk hedge)—this Shyamadi group (श्यामादि गण/black trivrit group) destroys skin diseases (कुष्ठ/skin disorders), poison (विष/toxins), anorexia (अरोचक/loss of appetite), kapha, heart disease (हृद्रुज्/cardiac pain), and dysuria (मूत्रकृच्छ्र/painful urination).
Pippali (पिप्पली/long pepper), Pippalimula (पिप्पलीमूल/pepper root), Chavya (चव्य/java long pepper), Chitraka (चित्रक/leadwort), Bhringavera Ginger (भृंगवेर/fresh ginger), Maricha (मरिच/black pepper), Hastipippali (हस्तिपिप्पली/elephant pepper), Harenuka (हरेणुक/pea seed), Ela (एला/cardamom), Ayamoda (अजमोद/celery seed), Indrayava (इंद्रयव/conessi seeds), Patha (पाठा/velvet leaf), Jeeraka (जीरक/cumin), Sarshapa (सर्षप/mustard), Mahanimba (महानिंब/bead tree), Phalgu (फल्गु/wild fig), Hingu (हिंगु/asafoetida), Bharangi (भार्ङ्गी/pygmy flower), Vacha (वचा/sweet flag), Musta (मुस्ता/nut grass), Madhurasa (मधुरसा/murcha), Ativisha (अतिविषा/aconite root), Vidanga (विडंग/false black pepper), Katurohini (कटुरोहिणी/picrorhiza).
This Pippalyadi group (पिप्पल्यादि गण/long pepper group) destroys kapha humor (कफ/phlegm), rhinitis (प्रतिश्याय/common cold), vata humor (अनिल/wind), and anorexia (अरोचक/loss of appetite).
It acts as appetite booster (दीपन/digestion booster), cures abdominal tumors (गुल्म/phantom tumor), abdominal colic (शूल/colic pain), and digests undigested chyme toxins (आमपाचन/chyme toxin digestion).
Thus twenty-five herb groups (पंचविंशति गण/twenty-five plant classes) are described; in case of non-availability of any drug, substitute similar drugs and omit non-fitting ones.
One should substitute similar herbal drugs (तद्विध द्रव्य/similar substitute) and leave out incompatible herbs carefully.
These herb groups, considering humor (दोष/bodily humor) and tissue (दूष्य/affected tissue), prepared as paste (कल्क/herbal paste), decoction (क्वाथ/herbal decoction), medicated oils/ghee (स्नेह/unctuous oil/fat), or confections (लेह/herbal confection),
Used internally for drinking, nasal drops (नस्य/nasal administration), enema (अन्वासन/unctuous enema), or externally as ointments (लेप/herbal paste application) and oil massage (अभ्यंग/oil massage), cure even most stubborn diseases (सुकृच्छ्र रोग/stubborn severe disease).
Thus ends the sixteenth chapter (षोडशः अध्यायः/sixteenth chapter) named 'Diversity of Herb Classes' (विविधगणसंग्रहः/diverse plant categories) of Ashtanga Sangraha.




Now, therefore, we shall explain the chapter (अध्याय/section) on the principles of substances, qualities, and tastes (द्रव्यादिविज्ञानीयः/knowledge of dravya and guna) of Ashtanga Sangraha.
