Sesame paste mixed with licorice and honey is lauded for wound healing.
Signs of Proper Cauterization (सम्यग् दग्ध/properly cauterized): lesion turns dark black like a ripe black plum (जम्बू फल/black plum), subsides, and pain ceases.
Signs of Deficient Cauterization (दुर्दग्ध/under-cauterized): itching, pricking pain, heaviness, requiring re-application of alkali.
Signs of Excessive Cauterization (अतिदग्ध/over-cauterized): severe burning, redness, inflammation, suppuration, tissue sloughing, fever, and spreading erysipelas.
Further complications of over-cauterization:
In eyes: eyelid destruction and loss of vision.
In nose: nasal septum destruction, stenosis, and loss of smell.
In ears: hearing loss and structural destruction.
In anus: anal stricture, urinary retention, severe diarrhea, impotence, or death from anal destruction.
Neutralizing excessive alkali immediately with repeated applications of sour liquids and cooling ghee.
Therefore, alkali should never be applied in excess or in a single massive dose.
Here are the explanatory verses:
Sour liquids are cooling to touch and rapidly neutralize the hot, sharp quality of alkali, transforming it into a sweet soothing state.
Therefore, alkali burns must be neutralized immediately with sour washes (अम्ल/sour wash).
Complications of uncontrolled alkali burns: fever, severe diarrhea, thirst, delirium, fainting, and chest pain, burning the body like wild fire.
Uncontrolled alkali burns burn the body like wild forest fire.
If alkali is accidentally swallowed in excess, quickly give ghee mixed with curd, jaggery, or sugar water.
Giving oil, ghee, and sugar water to drink.
Ghee boiled with double quantity of fresh juice of Amla, Kapittha, and sour pomegranate relieves severe alkali burn pain.
Washing and drinking this sour ghee recipe destroys severe pain from alkali excess.
Boiling sour juices of Pomegranate, Amla, Mango, Matulunga, and Kapittha over mild fire.
Administering this sour potion to neutralize remaining alkali according to strength.
It neutralizes alkali completely and restores natural skin complexion.
Alkali is neutralized thereby, and skin regains normal texture and complexion.
If excessive bleeding occurs, apply cooling internal and external styptic therapies.
Thus ends the thirty-ninth chapter (एकोनचत्वारिंशः अध्यायः/thirty-ninth chapter) named 'Chemical Cautery and Alkali Applications' (क्षारविधिः/alkali rules) of Ashtanga Sangraha.




Now, therefore, we shall explain the chapter (अध्याय/section) on thermal cauterization rules (अग्निकर्म विधि/cautery rules) of Ashtanga Sangraha.
Thermal cautery (अग्नि/thermal cautery) is even superior to alkali cautery (क्षार/alkali cautery) because diseases incurable by herbs, surgery, or alkali are cured by cautery and do not recur (अपुनर्भव/non-recurrence).
Thermal cauterization is applied on the skin (त्वच्/skin), flesh (मांस/muscle), blood vessels (सिरा/blood vessel), ligaments (स्नायु/ligament), joints (सन्धि/joint), and bones (अस्थि/bone).
For skin cauterization (त्वग्दाह/skin cautery) in moles (मश/mole), black spots (तिलकालक/freckle), warts (चर्मकील/skin wart), painful stiff emaciated limbs, conjunctivitis (अभिष्यन्द/conjunctivitis), glaucoma (अधिमन्थ/glaucoma), and headache over eyebrows, temples, and forehead, tools used include sun-gem glass (सूर्यकान्त/sun crystal), Long Pepper (पिप्पली/long pepper), goat dung (अजाशकृत्/goat dung), cow tooth (गौदन्त/cow tooth), arrow tips, and metallic rods over forehead/temple spots.
For flesh cauterization (मांसदाह/flesh cautery) in cysts (ग्रन्थि/glandular cyst), tumors (अर्बुद/tumor), hemorrhoids (अर्शस्/piles), fistula-in-ano (भगन्दर/fistula), goiter, scrofula (गण्डमाला/scrofula), elephantiasis (श्लीपद/elephantiasis), hernia (आन्त्रवृद्धि/inguinal hernia), infected ulcers, sinus tracks, and deep pus/lymph pockets, tools used include Jambavoushta (जाम्बवौष्ठ/plum-shaped iron rod), needles (सूची/needle), probes, heated ghee, jaggery, honey, liquorice paste, oils, muscle fat, and heated metallic rods of gold, copper, iron, silver, or bronze.
For vessel/ligament/joint/bone cauterization (सिरादिदाह/vessel cautery) in severed vessels, profuse bleeding (शोणितातित्रवृत्ति/excess bleeding), dental sinus, impacted eyelids, trichiasis (उपपक्ष्म/trichiasis), cataract (लिङ्गनाश/cataract), and improper vein puncture, tools used include Jambavoushta rods, probes, needles, melted beeswax (मधूच्छिष्ट/beeswax), honey, jaggery, and boiling unctuous oils.
Thermal cautery should not be performed in perforated abdomen (भिन्नकोष्ठ/ruptured abdomen), internal hemorrhage, retained internal foreign body, multiple painful wounds, hot summer season, or in patients unfit for alkali cautery.
However, in acute life-threatening medical emergencies (आत्ययिक व्याधि/emergency disease), cauterization may be done after counteracting seasonal heat and feeding slimy unctuous food.
The fit patient, after auspicious prayers, is laid with head facing east and held firmly by assistants; the physician heats Jambavoushta tools over smokeless red-hot coals of Khadira or Badara wood until glowing bright red, and applies cautery in patterns of ring (वलय/ring pattern), half-moon (अर्धचन्द्र/half-moon pattern), swastika (स्वस्तिक/cross pattern), chessboard (अष्टापद/grid pattern), dot (बिन्दु/dot pattern), line (रेखा/line pattern), or rubbing (प्रतिसारण/rubbing pattern) while comforting the patient with reassuring words and cold water until proper cautery signs appear.
In swollen, hollow, broken dental sinuses or worm-infested infected ulcers, fill the cavity with fat, beeswax, or jaggery before applying thermal cautery.
Upon proper cauterization, apply honey (मधु/honey) and ghee (सर्पिस्/ghee), followed by cooling unctuous herbal pastes.
General proper cauterization signs (सम्यग्दग्ध लक्षण/proper cautery signs) include crackling sound during burning, localized burnt odor, and skin contraction.
Proper skin cauterization (त्वग्दग्ध/skin cautery) produces a pigeon-gray color (कपोतवर्ण/pigeon gray), mild swelling and pain, and a dry shriveled wound.
Proper flesh cauterization (मांसदग्ध/flesh cautery) produces a black raised wound (कृष्णोन्नत व्रण/black raised wound), stoppage of bleeding, and slight watery lymph discharge.
Proper vessel and ligament cauterization (सिरादाह/vessel cautery) produces a dark reddish, rough, stable depressed wound.
Under-cauterization and over-cauterization symptoms and treatments are identical to accidental burn trauma (प्रमाददग्ध/accidental burn).
Accidental burn trauma (प्रमाददग्ध/accidental burn) is fourfold: singed/superficial (तुच्छ/singed burn), under-burnt (दुर्दग्ध/under-burnt), properly-burnt (सम्यग्दग्ध/proper burn), and over-burnt (अतिदग्ध/over-burnt).
Superficial singed burn (तुच्छ/singed burn) shows skin discoloration and severe localized burning sensation.
Under-burnt trauma (दुर्दग्ध/under-burnt) produces blisters (स्फोट/blister), intense heat, severe burning pain, and slow healing.
Proper burn (सम्यग्दग्ध/proper burn) appears ripe palmyra fruit-colored (तालाफलवर्ण/ripe palm fruit color), well-contoured, with classical proper cautery features.
Over-burnt trauma (अतिदग्ध/over-burnt) causes fierce pain, fuming feeling, sloughing flesh, vessel destruction, deep ulcers, fever, burning, thirst, fainting, and vomiting.
It triggers profuse secondary hemorrhage (शोणितातिप्रवृत्ति/profuse bleeding), systemic complications, difficult wound healing, and permanent skin discoloration.
Boiling oil/fat burns (स्नेहदाह/fat burn) are exceptionally severe because hot fat penetrates deeply through micro-channels into deeper tissues.
Here are the summarizing verses (भवति चात्र/summary verses):
For superficial singed burns, apply warm fomentation (अभितप्तपन/warm heat treatment) and warm herbal medications.
Because cold applications freeze trapped blood and prevent internal heat from escaping outward;
Consequently, pain increases and trapped blood undergoes severe internal burning;
Warm treatment draws internal heat outward and pacifies localized pain.
For under-burnt trauma, apply both cold and warm therapies sequentially;
Then treat with cooling ghee applications, pastes, and cold showers.
For proper burns, apply a paste of bamboo manna (तवक्षीरी/bamboo silica), red sandalwood, and red ochre (गैरिक/red ochre);
Mixed with Guduchi (अमृता/guduchi) and ghee, or apply marshy animal flesh paste.
Once burning heat subsides, treat the wound according to Pitta abscess (पित्तविद्रधि/pitta abscess) protocols.
For over-burnt trauma, remove sloughing dead flesh and administer cooling therapies;
Then apply a paste of pounded red Shali rice flour;
Or apply Tinduka bark decoction paste mixed with ghee;
Cover the burn wound with fresh leaves of Guduchi or Upodika;
Or treat all complications according to Pitta erysipelas (पित्तविसर्प/pitta erysipelas) management.
For severe boiling oil burns (स्नेहदग्ध/fat burn), apply dry (रूक्ष/dry) drying formulations intensely.
Because surgery, alkali, and thermal cautery are potential weapons of death if misused;
A wise physician must apply them with extreme vigilance (अप्रमत्त/vigilant) and care.
Thus ends the Sutrasthana (सूत्रस्थान/section on fundamental principles), the core heart of this treatise.
Here, subtle medical truths summarized in aphoristic verses are elaborated thoroughly in subsequent sections.
Thus ends the fortieth chapter (चत्वारिंशः अध्यायः/fortieth chapter) named 'Thermal Cauterization Rules' (अग्निकर्मविधिः/thermal cautery rules) of Ashtanga Sangraha.
Thus ends the first book, Sutrasthana (प्रथमं सूत्रस्थानम्/first section on core principles), composed by Acharya Vagbhata (वाग्भटाचार्य/Vagbhata), son of Sri Simhagupta.
May this work be dedicated as an offering unto Lord Shri Krishna (श्रीकृष्णार्पणम्/dedication to Krishna).
